</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprobate</td><td>/ˈreprəbeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堕落者，道德败坏的人</td><td>an old reprobate<br>一个老无赖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's hero is a charming reprobate with no money.  小说的主角是个迷人却身无分文的堕落者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 villain</td><td>/ˈvɪlən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>恶棍；反派人物</td><td>the villain of the story<br>故事里的反派</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every good film needs a villain the audience loves hating.  每部好片都需要一个让观众爱恨的反派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infraction</td><td>/ɪnˈfrækʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>违背，违犯</td><td>a minor infraction<br>轻微的违规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The referee stopped play to penalize a minor infraction near the goal.  裁判中断比赛，对球门附近的轻微违规进行判罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infringement</td><td>/ɪnˈfrɪndʒmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>侵犯，违反</td><td>copyright infringement<br>版权侵犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court ruled that the logo was a clear infringement of copyright.  法院裁定该标志明显侵犯了版权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 offense</td><td>/əˈfens/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>冒犯，得罪；犯罪</td><td>take offense at<br>因……而生气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took offense at his joke about her accent.  她因他拿她的口音开玩笑而生气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 peccadillo</del></td><td>/ˌpekəˈdɪloʊ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>小过失，小毛病</td><td>overlook his peccadilloes<br>忽略他的小过失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters forgave the mayor's peccadilloes because of his real achievements.  因为确有实绩，选民原谅了市长的小过失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 violation</td><td>/vaɪəˈleɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>违反，侵犯</td><td>a violation of the rules<br>违反规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parking here is a direct violation of the city ordinance.  在此停车直接违反了市政条例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cavalier</td><td>/ˌkævəˈlɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>有骑士风度的人；傲慢轻率的</td><td>a cavalier attitude<br>傲慢轻率的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company took a cavalier attitude toward worker safety.  这家公司对工人安全的态度傲慢轻率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 chevalier</td><td>/ˌʃevəˈlɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>骑士；有骑士风度的人</td><td>a French chevalier<br>一位法国骑士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chevalier still bowed like a courtier of Versailles.  这位老骑士仍像凡尔赛宫的朝臣那样行礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 felony</td><td>/ˈfeləni/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>重罪</td><td>convicted of a felony<br>被判重罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was convicted of a felony and lost his license.  他被判重罪，并被吊销了执照。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>miserly</b></td><td>/ˈmaɪzərli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a miserly tip<br>吝啬的小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He left a miserly tip after complaining about the service all evening.  他整晚抱怨服务，最后只留下吝啬的小费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 closefisted</del></td><td>/ˌkloʊsˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，一毛不拔的</td><td>a closefisted landlord<br>吝啬的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their closefisted landlord refused to fix the heater.  他们吝啬的房东拒绝修暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mean</td><td>/miːn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑贱的；吝啬的</td><td>a mean little room<br>简陋卑贱的小屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family lived in a mean little room above a noisy printing shop.  一家人住在嘈杂印刷店楼上一间简陋的小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 niggard</del></td><td>/ˈnɪɡərd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>吝啬鬼，小气鬼</td><td>a stingy old niggard<br>吝啬的老小气鬼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old niggard refused to spend a penny on heating.  这个老吝啬鬼连一分钱取暖费都不肯花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parsimonious</td><td>/ˌpɑːrsəˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，过分节俭的</td><td>parsimonious with praise<br>极少给出赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parsimonious landlord refused to replace the broken heater.  吝啬的房东拒绝更换坏了的暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 penurious</del></td><td>/pəˈnʊriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；吝啬的</td><td>penurious circumstances<br>贫困的境况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent a penurious youth in a crowded tenement.  他在拥挤的廉租房里度过了贫困的青年时期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stingy</td><td>/ˈstɪndʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>小气的，吝啬的</td><td>stingy with money<br>在钱上很吝啬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is stingy with money but surprisingly generous with his time.  他在钱上吝啬，在时间上却出奇大方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 tightfisted</del></td><td>/ˌtaɪtˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a tightfisted employer<br>吝啬的雇主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their tightfisted employer counted every paper clip.  他们吝啬的雇主连回形针都要数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lavish</td><td>/ˈlævɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>奢侈的，大量的，无节制的；挥霍，浪费</td><td>a lavish banquet<br>奢华的宴会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hosted a lavish banquet for three hundred guests in the garden.  他们在花园里为三百位客人举办了一场奢华的宴会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prodigal</td><td>/ˈprɑːdɪɡl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>挥霍的</td><td>prodigal spending<br>挥霍无度的开销</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His prodigal spending emptied the family fortune within a few years.  他挥霍无度的花钱方式几年内就耗尽了家产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 spendthrift</td><td>/ˈspendθrɪft/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>挥霍者，败家子</td><td>a reckless spendthrift<br>肆意挥霍的败家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A reckless spendthrift, he owed money to nearly every friend.  作为一个挥霍无度的人，他几乎欠了每个朋友的钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 squandering</td><td>/ˈskwɑːndərɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>浪费的，挥霍的</td><td>squandering public funds<br>挥霍公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Squandering money on gadgets left him broke by December.  在小玩意上挥霍钱财让他十二月就身无分文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>manacle</b></del></td><td>/ˈmænəkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…戴上手铐；束缚，限制</td><td>manacle the prisoner<br>给囚犯戴上手铐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy debts manacle the young couple, leaving them no freedom to change careers.  沉重的债务束缚了这对年轻夫妇，让他们没有转行的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chain</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>连接，用链系住；链条</td><td>chain the parts together<br>把各部分连起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He chained his bicycle to the railing outside the shop.  他把自行车锁在店外的栏杆上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 check</td><td>/tʃek/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使突然停止，阻止；检查，核对</td><td>check the spread of disease<br>阻止疾病蔓延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict quarantine measures checked the spread of the disease.  严格的隔离措施阻止了疾病的蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clog</td><td>/klɑːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>阻碍物</td><td>a clog on progress<br>进步的障碍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old regulations act as a clog on economic progress.  陈旧的法规成了经济进步的阻碍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encumber</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍；妨碍</td><td>encumber the project<br>妨碍这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many rules encumber small businesses in this country.  太多规定阻碍了这个国家的小企业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fetter</td><td>/ˈfetər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚</td><td>the fetters of tradition<br>传统的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy family obligations fettered her ambition for many long years.  沉重的家庭责任多年来束缚了她的雄心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hamper</td><td>/ˈhæmpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍；阻碍；束缚</td><td>hamper the rescue effort<br>妨碍救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow hampered the rescue teams trying to reach the stranded climbers.  大雪妨碍了试图接近被困登山者的救援队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handicap</td><td>/ˈhændikæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>妨碍，使不利；障碍</td><td>handicap the team<br>使球队处于不利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor equipment handicapped the researchers from the very start.  设备不足从一开始就妨碍了研究人员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 restrain</td><td>/rɪˈstreɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，约束，制止</td><td>restrain his temper<br>克制他的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She could hardly restrain her laughter during the speech.  演讲时她几乎忍不住笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shackle</td><td>/ˈʃækl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚；限制，阻碍</td><td>shackle free trade<br>束缚自由贸易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New tariffs would shackle trade between the two neighboring countries.  新关税会束缚这两个邻国之间的贸易。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trammel</td><td>/ˈtræməl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚，限制</td><td>the trammels of custom<br>习俗的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old rules trammel the creativity of young designers here.  旧规矩束缚了这里年轻设计师的创造力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enfranchise</td><td>/ɪnˈfræntʃaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给予…权利（例如选举权）；解放</td><td>enfranchise women<br>赋予妇女选举权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The amendment enfranchised millions of citizens for the first time.  该修正案首次赋予数百万公民选举权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberate</td><td>/ˈlɪbəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解放，释放</td><td>liberate the city<br>解放这座城市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Allied troops liberated the city in the spring of that year.  盟军在那年春天解放了这座城市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unbind</td><td>/ʌnˈbaɪnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开，松开，释放</td><td>unbind the prisoner<br>解开囚犯的绑缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She knelt down to unbind the wounded man's hands.  她跪下来解开那名伤者被绑的双手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unfetter</del></td><td>/ʌnˈfɛtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解除束缚，使自由</td><td>unfetter the market<br>解除对市场的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform unfettered small firms and let them compete freely.  改革解除了对小企业的束缚，让它们自由竞争。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unshackle</td><td>/ʌnˈʃækəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解开束缚，释放</td><td>unshackle the economy<br>解除对经济的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reforms unshackled small businesses from needless paperwork.  改革让小企业摆脱了无谓的文书负担。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>misgiving</b></td><td>/ˌmɪsˈɡɪvɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>担忧，疑虑</td><td>have serious misgivings<br>深感疑虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had serious misgivings about signing the contract without a lawyer present.  她对在没有律师在场的情况下签合同深感疑虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apprehension</td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhenʃn/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>忧虑，担心；逮捕</td><td>a sense of apprehension<br>忧虑感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She waited for the results with growing apprehension.  她越来越忧虑地等待结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distrust</td><td>/dɪsˈtrʌst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>不信任，怀疑</td><td>deep distrust of authority<br>对权威的深深不信任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many villagers still distrust the promises made by developers.  许多村民仍不信任开发商的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doubt</td><td>/ˈdaʊt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>怀疑，疑虑</td><td>cast doubt on<br>使人怀疑…</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is little doubt that the data was carelessly recorded.  几乎可以肯定，这些数据记录得很草率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dread</td><td>/dred/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恐惧</td><td>fill him with dread<br>使他充满恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thought of another interview filled him with dread all weekend.  一想到还要再面试一次，他整个周末都充满恐惧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fear</td><td>/ˈfɪr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>恐惧，害怕</td><td>fear of failure<br>对失败的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His fear of speaking in public gradually faded with practice.  他对公开演讲的恐惧随练习逐渐消退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foreboding</td><td>/fɔːrˈboʊdɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不祥的；不祥的预感</td><td>a sense of foreboding<br>不祥的预感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt a strange foreboding as the sky darkened.  天色转暗时她有种奇怪的不祥预感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incertitude</del></td><td>/ɪnˈsɜːrtɪtuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不确定，疑虑</td><td>a state of incertitude<br>疑虑不定的状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Months of incertitude about funding paralyzed the whole project.  数月对资金的不确定使整个项目停滞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skepticism</td><td>/ˈskeptɪˌsɪzəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>怀疑，怀疑态度</td><td>widespread public skepticism<br>普遍的公众怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Scientists greeted the claim with polite but obvious skepticism.  科学家对该说法表现出礼貌却明显的怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suspicion</td><td>/səˈspɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>怀疑，猜疑</td><td>arouse suspicion<br>引起怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sudden wealth aroused the suspicion of the neighbors.  她突然的财富引起了邻居的怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assurance</td><td>/əˈʃʊrəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>保证；自信，确信</td><td>give an assurance<br>作出保证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered the difficult questions with surprising assurance.  她以惊人的自信回答了那些难题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 belief</td><td>/bɪˈliːf/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>信念，相信</td><td>a firm belief<br>坚定的信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her belief in the project never wavered, even after the setbacks.  即使遭遇挫折，她对项目的信念也从未动摇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 certainty</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtənti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>确定，必然；确信</td><td>with absolute certainty<br>绝对确定地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one can predict the outcome with absolute certainty.  没人能绝对确定地预测结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 certitude</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtɪtuːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>确信无疑</td><td>moral certitude<br>道德上的确信</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spoke of the plan's success with unshakable certitude.  他以不可动摇的确信谈起这项计划的成功。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 confidence</td><td>/ˈkɑːnfədəns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>信心，确信</td><td>full of confidence<br>充满信心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered every question with quiet confidence.  他从容而自信地回答了每个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conviction</td><td>/kənˈvɪkʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>深信，确信；证明有罪</td><td>speak with conviction<br>满怀信念地说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She argued her case with a conviction that silenced every critic.  她以坚定的信念陈述自己的主张，令所有批评者沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sureness</td><td>/ˈʃʊrnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>确信；稳准</td><td>the sureness of his touch<br>他手法的稳准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She played the difficult passage with remarkable sureness.  她极其稳准地演奏了那个难段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surety</td><td>/ˈʃʊrəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>担保人；确信</td><td>stand surety for someone<br>为某人担保</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His uncle agreed to act as surety for the loan.  他叔叔同意为这笔贷款作担保。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trust</td><td>/trʌst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>信任，信赖</td><td>win someone's trust<br>赢得某人信任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It takes years to build trust and one lie to lose it.  建立信任要数年，一句谎言就足以失去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mishap</b></td><td>/ˈmɪshæp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>小事故；不幸之事</td><td>a minor mishap<br>小事故</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A minor mishap in the kitchen delayed dinner by nearly an hour.  厨房里的一个小事故让晚饭推迟了将近一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adversity</td><td>/ədˈvɜːrsəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>厄运，逆境</td><td>face adversity bravely<br>勇敢面对逆境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He faced years of adversity without ever losing his sense of humor.  他多年身处逆境，却从未失去幽默感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 calamity</td><td>/kəˈlæməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>灾难，大祸；不幸事件</td><td>a national calamity<br>国家性的大灾难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The flood was the worst calamity the small town had faced.  这场洪水是这个小镇遭遇过的最严重灾难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cataclysm</td><td>/ˈkætəklɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大灾难；剧变</td><td>a social cataclysm<br>社会剧变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The eruption was a cataclysm that reshaped the entire island.  那次喷发是一场重塑整座岛屿的大灾难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 catastrophe</td><td>/kəˈtæstrəfi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大灾难；彻底的失败</td><td>an ecological catastrophe<br>生态大灾难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The oil spill became an ecological catastrophe for the whole coast.  漏油事件成为整个海岸的生态大灾难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disaster</td><td>/dɪˈzæstər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>灾难；彻底的失败</td><td>a natural disaster<br>自然灾害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The opening night was a disaster from the very first scene.  首演之夜从第一场戏起就是一场彻底的失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ill</td><td>/ˈɪl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>祸害，弊病；生病的</td><td>social ills<br>社会弊病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report lists the ills afflicting the health system.  报告列出了困扰医疗系统的种种弊病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mischance</td><td>/mɪsˈtʃæns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不幸，厄运</td><td>by sheer mischance<br>纯属不幸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By sheer mischance the letter arrived a day too late.  纯属不幸，信迟到了一天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misfortune</td><td>/mɪsˈfɔːrtʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不幸，灾祸</td><td>suffer a misfortune<br>遭遇不幸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A series of misfortunes left the family deeply in debt.  一连串的不幸让这家人负债累累。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tragedy</td><td>/ˈtrædʒədi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>惨事，灾难；悲剧</td><td>a terrible tragedy<br>一场可怕的惨剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mine collapse was a tragedy that the town never forgot.  矿井塌方是这座小镇永难忘却的惨剧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortune</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>运气；财富</td><td>a stroke of good fortune<br>一次好运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stroke of good fortune saved the failing bakery.  一次好运救了这家濒临倒闭的面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 luck</td><td>/lʌk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>运气，幸运</td><td>a run of bad luck<br>一连串坏运气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pure luck brought them to the only open shelter.  纯凭运气他们找到了唯一开放的避难所。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 serendipity</td><td>/ˌserənˈdɪpəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>意外发现珍奇（或称心）事物的本领</td><td>a moment of serendipity<br>意外收获的时刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pure serendipity led the researchers to a cure they were not seeking.  纯粹的意外机缘让研究者找到了他们并未寻求的疗法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>misrepresent</b></td><td>/ˌmɪsˌreprɪˈzent/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误传，篡改</td><td>misrepresent the facts<br>歪曲事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics accused the report of misrepresenting the facts to favor one side.  批评者指责这份报告歪曲事实以偏向一方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 belie</td><td>/bɪˈlaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>与…相对立；与…相矛盾</td><td>belie his claims<br>与他的说法矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company's own records belie his claims about the accident.  公司自己的记录与他关于事故的说法相矛盾。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 color</td><td>/ˈkʌlər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>渲染，影响（叙述）；着色</td><td>color the account<br>渲染这段叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His prejudice clearly colored the report he submitted to us.  他的偏见明显影响了他交给我们的报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distort</td><td>/dɪˈstɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扭曲，歪曲</td><td>distort the facts<br>歪曲事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Editing the tape that way would distort the facts of the interview.  那样剪辑录音会歪曲采访的事实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 falsify</td><td>/ˈfɔːlsəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>篡改，伪造；证明为假</td><td>falsify the records<br>篡改记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An accountant was accused of falsifying the company's records.  一名会计被指控篡改公司账目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garble</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>曲解，篡改，混淆（以至使无法理解）</td><td>garble the message<br>曲解这条消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A bad connection garbled the message beyond all understanding.  信号不好把这条消息混淆得完全无法理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misinterpret</td><td>/ˌmɪsɪnˈtɜːrprɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误解，误读</td><td>misinterpret the data<br>误读数据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers often misinterpret irony as sincere praise.  读者常把讽刺误解为真诚的赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 misrelate</del></td><td>/ˌmɪsrɪˈleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>错误叙述</td><td>misrelate the facts<br>错述事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Witnesses often misrelate details of events they saw only briefly.  证人常错述他们匆匆看到的细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 misstate</del></td><td>/mɪsˈsteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>错误陈述，误报</td><td>misstate the figures<br>错报数字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company was accused of misstating its annual earnings.  该公司被指错报年度收益。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pervert</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>曲解，使颠倒；使堕落</td><td>pervert the course of justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawyers accused him of trying to pervert the truth in court.  律师指控他试图在法庭上曲解真相。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clarify</td><td>/ˈklerɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明，澄清</td><td>clarify the rules<br>澄清规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dean clarified the rules after weeks of confused complaints.  在数周混乱的投诉后，院长澄清了规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 explain</td><td>/ɪkˈspleɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>解释，说明</td><td>explain the reason<br>解释原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you explain why the experiment failed the second time?  你能解释一下第二次实验为何失败吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illuminate</td><td>/ɪˈluːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>illuminate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lecture illuminated a problem that had puzzled economists for years.  她的讲座阐明了一个困扰经济学家多年的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illustrate</td><td>/ˈɪləsˌtreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>说明，举例说明</td><td>illustrate the point<br>说明这一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These figures illustrate how quickly the population has grown.  这些数字说明人口增长得多么迅速。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mite</b></td><td>/maɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>微小的东西，很少的钱</td><td>a mite of comfort<br>一点点安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His brief letter offered a mite of comfort during those difficult winter months.  他简短的信在那些艰难的冬月里带来了一点点安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 atom</td><td>/ˈætəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>原子；微量</td><td>not an atom of truth<br>毫无一丝真实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is not an atom of evidence for that wild claim.  这一荒诞说法没有一丝证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bit</td><td>/ˈbɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一点，少量</td><td>a bit of luck<br>一点运气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Add just a bit of salt to the simmering sauce.  往煮着的酱汁里加一点点盐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hint</td><td>/ˈhɪnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>暗示，提示</td><td>drop a hint<br>透露一点暗示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave me a subtle hint about the surprise party.  她微妙地暗示了那个惊喜派对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 iota</td><td>/aɪˈoʊtə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极少量，一点</td><td>not one iota<br>一点也不</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has not changed one iota since we last met.  自上次见面他一点也没变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 molecule</td><td>/ˈmɑːləkˌjuːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分子；微小的量</td><td>a water molecule<br>一个水分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every water molecule contains two hydrogen atoms.  每个水分子含有两个氢原子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 particle</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>微粒；一点点</td><td>a particle of dust<br>一粒灰尘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Not a particle of dust was left on the polished shelf.  擦亮的架子上一粒灰尘也没剩下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pittance</td><td>/ˈpɪtns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量津贴</td><td>work for a pittance<br>为微薄报酬工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The apprentices worked long hours for a pittance and no benefits.  学徒们长时间工作，只拿微薄津贴且没有福利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trace</td><td>/ˈtreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>痕迹；微量</td><td>a trace of irony<br>一丝讽意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tea showed only a trace of bitterness.  这茶只有一丝苦味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 boodle</td><td>/ˈbuːdəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>赃款；一大堆钱</td><td>a whole boodle of cash<br>一大堆现金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The gang hid the boodle in an abandoned barn.  这帮人把赃款藏在废弃谷仓里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bundle</td><td>/ˈbʌndəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>匆忙送走，急送</td><td>bundle him into a car<br>把他匆匆塞进车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guards bundled the prisoner into a waiting van.  卫兵把犯人匆匆塞进等候的货车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortune</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>运气；财富</td><td>a stroke of good fortune<br>一次好运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stroke of good fortune saved the failing bakery.  一次好运救了这家濒临倒闭的面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mitigate</b></td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exacerbate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæsərbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧，使恶化</td><td>exacerbate the crisis<br>使危机加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the budget would only exacerbate the housing shortage.  削减预算只会使住房短缺进一步恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intensify</td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>majestic</b></td><td>/məˈdʒestɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宏伟壮丽的</td><td>majestic mountains<br>雄伟的群山</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The majestic peaks rose above the clouds in the morning light.  雄伟的山峰在晨光中耸立于云端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 august</td><td>/ɔːˈɡʌst/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>威严的，庄重的</td><td>an august assembly<br>庄严的集会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The august assembly of scholars rose when the old president entered.  当老校长进场时，这个威严的学者集会全体起立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 epic</td><td>/ˈepɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宏大的，超凡脱俗的</td><td>an epic journey<br>宏大的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their epic journey across the desert took nearly four months on foot.  他们徒步穿越沙漠的宏大旅程花了近四个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 glorious</td><td>/ˈɡlɔːriəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>辉煌的，壮丽的</td><td>a glorious summer sunset<br>壮丽的夏日落日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mountains looked glorious in the late afternoon light.  傍晚的光线下群山显得壮丽辉煌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grandiose</td><td>/ˈɡrændioʊs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>自命不凡的，浮夸的</td><td>grandiose plans<br>浮夸的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His grandiose plans for the company never survived contact with reality.  他为公司制定的浮夸计划一遇现实便破灭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imposing</td><td>/ɪmˈpoʊzɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宏伟壮丽的</td><td>an imposing building<br>宏伟的建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An imposing stone building dominates the square at the city center.  一座气势宏伟的石砌建筑主宰着市中心的广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnificent</td><td>/mæɡˈnɪfɪsnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>壮丽的</td><td>a magnificent view<br>壮丽的景色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the summit we enjoyed a magnificent view of the whole valley.  从山顶我们欣赏到整个山谷的壮丽景色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monumental</td><td>/ˌmɑːnjəˈmentəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>意义重大的；巨大的</td><td>a monumental achievement<br>意义重大的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building the cathedral was a monumental task that took two centuries.  建造这座大教堂是耗时两个世纪的浩大工程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 splendid</td><td>/ˈsplendɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>壮丽的；极好的</td><td>a splendid marble hall<br>壮丽的大理石厅堂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the roof we had a splendid view of the harbor.  从屋顶望去，港口的景色极为壮丽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unheroic</del></td><td>/ˌʌnhɪˈroʊɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非英勇的，平庸的</td><td>an unheroic retreat<br>不光彩的撤退</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unheroic decision to flee disappointed everyone who had trusted him.  他不英勇的逃跑决定让所有信任他的人失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unimposing</del></td><td>/ˌʌnɪmˈpoʊzɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不起眼的，不威严的</td><td>an unimposing building<br>不起眼的建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unimposing office hid one of the city's best libraries.  这不起眼的办公楼藏着城中最好的图书馆之一。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unimpressive</td><td>/ˌʌnɪmˈpresɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平淡无奇的，不起眼的</td><td>an unimpressive debut<br>平淡的首次登场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unimpressive debut gave no hint of the fame ahead.  他平淡的首次登场丝毫看不出日后的名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mockery</b></td><td>/ˈmɑːkəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>笑柄；嘲弄，拙劣的模仿</td><td>make a mockery of justice<br>使正义成为笑柄</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rushed trial made a mockery of justice in the eyes of observers.  在观察者看来，这场草率的审判使正义成了笑柄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 derision</td><td>/dɪˈrɪʒən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>嘲笑，嘲弄</td><td>an object of derision<br>嘲笑的对象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His proposal met with open derision from the board.  他的提议遭到董事会公开嘲笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 joke</td><td>/ˈdʒoʊk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>笑话；玩笑</td><td>a bad joke<br>一个糟糕的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole election became a joke to foreign observers.  在外国观察者看来，整场选举变成了笑话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mock</td><td>/ˈmɑːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>假的，模拟的；嘲笑</td><td>a mock trial<br>模拟审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students held a mock election to learn how voting works.  学生举行模拟选举以了解投票如何运作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ridicule</td><td>/ˈrɪdɪkjuːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>嘲笑；讥讽；愚弄</td><td>an object of ridicule<br>被嘲笑的对象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Classmates began to ridicule him for his old shoes and worn coat.  同学们开始因他的旧鞋和破外套而嘲笑他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scoffing</td><td>/ˈskɔfɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>嘲笑的；嘲笑</td><td>a scoffing tone<br>嘲笑的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dismissed the proposal in a scoffing tone.  他用嘲笑的语气否定了这项提议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 burlesque</td><td>/bɜːrˈlesk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>滑稽夸张的模仿讽刺作品；恶搞</td><td>a burlesque of politics<br>对政治的滑稽恶搞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play is a clever burlesque of modern political debate.  这部戏是对当代政治辩论的巧妙滑稽模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caricature</td><td>/ˈkærɪkətʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>讽刺画，夸张的滑稽模仿</td><td>draw a caricature<br>画一幅讽刺画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cartoonist drew a cruel caricature of the new mayor.  漫画家画了一幅刻薄的新市长讽刺画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 farce</td><td>/fɑːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>闹剧；滑稽戏；荒唐事</td><td>turn into a farce<br>变成一场闹剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial soon turned into a farce that embarrassed the whole court.  这场审判很快变成一场闹剧，让整个法庭都很难堪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parody</td><td>/ˈpærədi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（以嘲笑原作作者的）模仿作品，恶搞</td><td>a parody of the film<br>对该片的恶搞模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students wrote a hilarious parody of the morning speech at assembly.  学生们写了一篇滑稽的作品，恶搞集会上的早间讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sham</td><td>/ʃæm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>掩饰，假装；虚假的</td><td>a sham marriage<br>假婚姻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The couple admitted their marriage was a sham arranged for the visa.  这对夫妻承认他们的婚姻是为签证安排的假婚姻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 travesty</td><td>/ˈtrævəsti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拙劣的、嘲弄性模仿</td><td>a travesty of justice<br>对正义的拙劣讽刺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics called the hasty trial a travesty of justice.  批评者称这场匆忙的审判是对正义的拙劣讽刺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 respect</td><td>/rɪˈspekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>尊敬，敬重；方面</td><td>show respect for<br>对……表示尊敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students should learn to respect opinions that differ from their own.  学生应学会尊重与自己不同的意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reverence</td><td>/ˈrevərəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>崇敬，尊崇</td><td>deep reverence for nature<br>对自然的深切崇敬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old scholar is treated with reverence by his many students.  这位老学者受到众多学生的崇敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 veneration</td><td>/ˌvenəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>崇敬，敬仰</td><td>an object of veneration<br>崇敬的对象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ancestors are held in veneration throughout much of East Asia.  在东亚许多地区，祖先受到崇敬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>moderate</b></td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abate</td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diminish</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）减少；轻视，贬低</td><td>diminish in importance<br>重要性下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence diminished steadily after he left the board.  他离开董事会后影响力持续减弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dwindle</td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lessen</td><td>/ˈlesən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减少，减轻</td><td>lessen the risk<br>减少风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple exercises lessen the pain in his stiff shoulder.  简单的锻炼减轻了他僵硬肩膀的疼痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lower</td><td>/ˈloʊər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>降低，减少；较低的</td><td>lower the temperature<br>降低温度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bank lowered interest rates twice in one year.  银行一年内两次降低利率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modulate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdʒəleɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>调节，调整</td><td>modulate one's voice<br>调节嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She learned to modulate her voice for the small room.  她学会为这小房间调节音量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subside</td><td>/səbˈsaɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>减弱，平息；下沉</td><td>the pain finally subsided<br>疼痛终于减轻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise subsided as soon as the teacher walked in.  老师一进来，喧闹声就平息了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taper</td><td>/ˈteɪpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>逐渐减少，减弱</td><td>taper off gradually<br>逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The storm tapered off gradually as the evening wore on.  随着夜色渐深，暴风雨逐渐平息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temper</td><td>/ˈtempər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>缓和，调节；脾气</td><td>temper justice with mercy<br>以仁慈缓和公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge tempered strict justice with unexpected mercy.  法官以意外的仁慈缓和了严苛的公正。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 average</td><td>/ˈævərɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平庸的，一般的；平均数</td><td>an average student<br>成绩平平的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His work is average but it always arrives on time.  他的活儿一般，但总能按时交。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conservative</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>守旧的，不愿改变的；不招摇的，低调的</td><td>a conservative estimate<br>保守的估计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were too conservative to accept such an unconventional career choice.  她的父母太守旧，无法接受这样不合常规的职业选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fair</td><td>/ˈfer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的；相当的；晴朗的</td><td>a fair trial<br>公正的审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone deserves a fair hearing before any decision is made.  在做出任何决定之前，人人都应得到公正的申辩机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intermediate</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmiːdiət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>中间的；中间物</td><td>an intermediate stage<br>中间阶段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reaction produces an intermediate compound that quickly breaks down again.  这个反应产生一种很快又分解的中间化合物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mediocre</td><td>/ˌmiːdiˈoʊkər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平庸的；质量中等偏下的</td><td>a mediocre performance<br>平庸的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The orchestra's mediocre performance disappointed listeners who had waited months for the concert.  乐团平庸的表演让等了几个月的听众很失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modest</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>谦虚的；不大的，适度的</td><td>modest about her success<br>对成功很谦虚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed modest about his award and thanked his team.  他对自己的奖项很谦虚，还感谢了团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temperate</td><td>/ˈtempərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言行举止）有分寸的，有节制的</td><td>a temperate response<br>有分寸的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a temperate reply to a very rude question.  面对无礼的问题她的回答很有分寸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 escalate</td><td>/ˈeskəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）（战争等）升级，扩大</td><td>escalate into violence<br>升级为暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border dispute quickly escalated into a full-scale armed conflict.  边界争端迅速升级为全面武装冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heighten</td><td>/ˈhaɪtən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加强，加剧</td><td>heighten the tension<br>加剧紧张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long silence only heightened everyone's sense of dread.  长久的沉默只是加剧了众人的恐惧感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intensify</td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exorbitant</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːrbɪtənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的</td><td>exorbitant prices<br>过高的价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourists complained about exorbitant prices for bottled water near the temple.  游客抱怨寺庙附近瓶装水价格过高。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 excessive</td><td>/ɪkˈsesɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过度的，过分的</td><td>excessive drinking<br>过量饮酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors warned him about his excessive salt intake.  医生警告他盐摄入过量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>modicum</b></td><td>/ˈmɑːdɪkəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量</td><td>a modicum of success<br>些许成功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 With a modicum of luck they finished the repairs before the storm arrived.  凭着些许运气，他们在暴风雨来临前完成了修理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 atom</td><td>/ˈætəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>原子；微量</td><td>not an atom of truth<br>毫无一丝真实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is not an atom of evidence for that wild claim.  这一荒诞说法没有一丝证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 iota</td><td>/aɪˈoʊtə/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>极少量，一点</td><td>not one iota<br>一点也不</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has not changed one iota since we last met.  自上次见面他一点也没变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mite</td><td>/maɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>微小的东西，很少的钱</td><td>a mite of comfort<br>一点点安慰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His brief letter offered a mite of comfort during those difficult winter months.  他简短的信在那些艰难的冬月里带来了一点点安慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 molecule</td><td>/ˈmɑːləkˌjuːl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>分子；微小的量</td><td>a water molecule<br>一个水分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every water molecule contains two hydrogen atoms.  每个水分子含有两个氢原子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 particle</td><td>/ˈpɑːrtəkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>微粒；一点点</td><td>a particle of dust<br>一粒灰尘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Not a particle of dust was left on the polished shelf.  擦亮的架子上一粒灰尘也没剩下。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abundance</td><td>/əˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>an abundance of fresh fruit<br>大量新鲜水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered an abundance of fresh fruit at low prices.  市场以低价供应大量新鲜水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 affluence</td><td>/ˈæfluəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>富裕，丰富</td><td>live in affluence<br>生活富裕</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family had lived in quiet affluence for three generations.  这家人已在平静的富裕中生活了三代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mollify</b></td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appease</td><td>/əˈpiːz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；满足</td><td>appease the angry crowd<br>安抚愤怒的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered refunds to appease its angry customers.  公司提供退款以安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conciliate</td><td>/kənˈsɪlieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；调解</td><td>conciliate angry customers<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager tried to conciliate the angry customers with refunds.  经理试图用退款来安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 placate</td><td>/ˈpleɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过让步以）平息抚慰</td><td>placate the critics<br>平息批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered full refunds to placate its angriest customers.  公司提供全额退款以平息最愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propitiate</td><td>/prəˈpɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抚慰，劝解</td><td>propitiate the angry gods<br>抚慰愤怒的神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers offered rice and flowers to propitiate the mountain spirits.  村民献上米和花来抚慰山神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enrage</td><td>/enˈreɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使愤怒，激怒</td><td>enrage the audience<br>激怒观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippant apology only enraged the families further.  他轻率的道歉只让那些家庭更加愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exasperate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæspəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使恼怒，激怒</td><td>exasperate his parents<br>使他的父母恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His constant lateness exasperated even the most patient teachers.  他总是迟到，连最有耐心的老师都被激怒了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incense</td><td>/ɪnˈsens/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>incense the public<br>激怒公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal is certain to incense feminists across the country.  这项提案肯定会激怒全国的女权主义者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflame</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧</td><td>inflame public anger<br>使公众愤怒加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless comments only inflamed public anger over the housing shortage.  市长轻率的言论只是加剧了公众对住房短缺的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infuriate</td><td>/ɪnˈfjʊrieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>infuriate the critics<br>激怒批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision to cut pensions infuriated thousands of workers across the country.  削减养老金的决定激怒了全国成千上万的工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ire</td><td>/ˈaɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>愤怒，怒火</td><td>draw the ire of critics<br>引来批评者的怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax drew the ire of small business owners.  新税收招致了小企业主的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rankle</td><td>/ˈræŋkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>rankle in his mind<br>在他心里作痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unfair remark continued to rankle him for many years afterward.  那句不公平的评论此后多年一直令他愤懑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 vex</td><td>/veks/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使烦恼，使恼怒</td><td>vex the teacher<br>使老师恼怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Constant interruptions vexed the teacher throughout the long afternoon lesson.  不断的打扰让老师在漫长的下午课上十分恼怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mollycoddle</b></td><td>/ˈmɑːlikɑːdl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宠爱，溺爱</td><td>mollycoddle the students<br>溺爱学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Some parents mollycoddle their children and leave them unprepared for real difficulties.  有些父母溺爱孩子，让他们无法应对真正的困难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coddle</td><td>/ˈkɑːdəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>娇养，溺爱</td><td>coddle a spoiled child<br>娇养被宠坏的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coaches should not coddle athletes who refuse to train hard.  教练不该娇养那些不肯苦练的运动员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cosset</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːsɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宠爱</td><td>cosset an only child<br>宠爱独生子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Grandparents tend to cosset the youngest child in the family.  祖父母往往宠爱家里最小的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indulge</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌldʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>纵容，迁就</td><td>indulge his every whim<br>纵容他的任性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Grandparents often indulge children in ways parents will not.  祖辈常以父母不会的方式纵容孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pamper</td><td>/ˈpæmpər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>纵容，娇养</td><td>pamper a child<br>娇养孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Grandparents love to pamper the children with sweets.  祖辈爱用糖果娇宠孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spoil</td><td>/ˈspɔɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>损坏，破坏；溺爱</td><td>spoil the surprise<br>破坏惊喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy rain spoiled the picnic we had planned for weeks.  大雨破坏了我们筹划数周的野餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abuse</td><td>/əˈbjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辱骂，抨击；不正当或不合理使用</td><td>abuse of power<br>滥用权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of abusing his position for profit.  他被指控滥用职位谋利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-treat</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈtriːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>虐待</td><td>ill-treat animals<br>虐待动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The farm was fined for ill-treating its animals.  该农场因虐待动物被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>molt</b></td><td>/moʊlt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>脱（羽、皮等）</td><td>molt in the spring<br>春季换羽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of these birds molt in the spring and look scruffy for weeks.  这些鸟大多在春季换羽，好几周都显得邋遢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exfoliate</td><td>/eksˈfoʊlieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>去除表层；脱皮</td><td>exfoliate dead skin<br>去除死皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This cream helps exfoliate the dry skin on your elbows.  这款霜有助去除肘部干皮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 exuviate</del></td><td>/ɪɡˈzuːvieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>蜕皮，蜕壳</td><td>exuviate its old skin<br>蜕去旧皮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The snake will exuviate its old skin within a few days.  这条蛇几天内就会蜕去旧皮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shed</td><td>/ˈʃed/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>摆脱，去掉；脱落</td><td>shed excess weight<br>减去多余体重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company shed three divisions to survive the downturn.  公司剥离了三个部门以度过衰退。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fledge</td><td>/ˈfledʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>长齐羽毛；养育至能飞</td><td>fledge in three weeks<br>三周后长齐羽毛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young swallows fledge about three weeks after hatching.  雏燕孵出约三周后长齐羽毛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>momentous</b></td><td>/moʊˈmentəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重大的，重要的</td><td>a momentous decision<br>一个重大的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board reached a momentous decision that changed the company's direction entirely.  董事会做出了一个重大决定，彻底改变了公司的方向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consequential</td><td>/ˌkɑːnsəˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；随之而来的</td><td>a consequential ruling<br>一项重要的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That quiet vote proved the most consequential act of the session.  那次不起眼的投票成了本届会议最重要的举动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 considerable</td><td>/kənˈsɪdərəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（数量上）可观（而值得注意）的</td><td>a considerable sum<br>一笔可观的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The renovation required a considerable amount of money and nearly two years of work.  这次翻修耗费了可观的资金和近两年的工期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crucial</td><td>/ˈkruːʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常重要的，决定性的</td><td>a crucial decision<br>关键的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The next two weeks will be crucial to the project's survival.  接下来两周对这个项目的存亡至关重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eventful</td><td>/ɪˈventfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多事的，重要的</td><td>an eventful year<br>多事的一年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It has been an eventful year for the whole family.  对全家来说这是多事的一年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 important</td><td>/ˌɪmˈpɔːrtənt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的</td><td>an important decision<br>一个重要的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sleep is as important as diet for a young athlete.  对年轻运动员来说，睡眠和饮食一样重要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monumental</td><td>/ˌmɑːnjəˈmentəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>意义重大的；巨大的</td><td>a monumental achievement<br>意义重大的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Building the cathedral was a monumental task that took two centuries.  建造这座大教堂是耗时两个世纪的浩大工程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pivotal</td><td>/ˈpɪvətl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>最关键的，最为重要的</td><td>play a pivotal role<br>起关键作用</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her testimony played a pivotal role in the outcome of the trial.  她的证词对审判结果起了关键作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 significant</td><td>/səɡˈnɪfɪkənt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的；显著的</td><td>a significant difference<br>显著的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers found a significant drop in the pollution levels.  研究者发现污染水平显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vital</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满活力的；极重要的</td><td>full of vital energy<br>充满生命活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise keeps older people vital and mentally alert.  经常锻炼使老年人保持活力和头脑清醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weighty</td><td>/ˈweɪti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的；沉重的</td><td>weighty matters<br>重大的事务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The elders debated weighty questions of land and water.  长者们讨论着土地与水源的重大问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconsequential</td><td>/ˌɪnkɑːnsɪˈkwenʃəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不重要的，微不足道的</td><td>an inconsequential remark<br>无关紧要的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The difference between the two prices is quite inconsequential.  两个价格之间的差别微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 negligible</td><td>/ˈneɡlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，可忽略的</td><td>a negligible difference<br>可忽略的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra cost is negligible compared with the savings.  与节省的钱相比，额外成本微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trifling</td><td>/ˈtraɪflɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，不重要的</td><td>a trifling sum<br>微不足道的一笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He dismissed the missing coins as a trifling sum not worth reporting.  他把丢失的硬币当作微不足道的小钱，不值得上报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trivial</td><td>/ˈtrɪviəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无足轻重的，不重要的</td><td>a trivial detail<br>无关紧要的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor refused to argue over such a trivial difference in wording.  编辑不愿为措辞上如此无足轻重的差别争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>momentum</b></td><td>/moʊˈmentəm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>动力；势头</td><td>gather momentum<br>积聚势头</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign gathered momentum after the debate and won three states easily.  这场竞选在辩论后积聚了势头，轻松赢下三个州。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boost</td><td>/ˈbuːst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提高，促进；推动</td><td>boost sales<br>促进销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax cut is expected to boost consumer spending.  减税预计会促进消费者支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encouragement</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>鼓励，激励</td><td>words of encouragement<br>鼓励的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A few words of encouragement kept the young runner going.  几句鼓励的话让这位小跑者坚持下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 goad</td><td>/ɡoʊd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>刺激，驱使，激发</td><td>goad him into action<br>激他采取行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His teammates goaded him into accepting the ridiculous bet.  队友们激他接受了那个荒唐的赌约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impetus</td><td>/ˈɪmpətəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>推动力，促进因素</td><td>give fresh impetus to<br>为……提供新动力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The grant gave fresh impetus to their stalled research.  这笔资助为他们停滞的研究提供了新动力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incentive</td><td>/ɪnˈsentɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激，诱因</td><td>a tax incentive<br>税收上的激励</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tax breaks gave farmers a strong incentive to plant native trees.  税收减免给了农民种植本土树木的强烈诱因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 incitation</del></td><td>/ˌɪnsaɪˈteɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动，刺激</td><td>incitation to violence<br>煽动暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlet was banned as an incitation to violence.  这本小册子因煽动暴力被禁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigation</td><td>/ˌɪnstɪˈɡeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>煽动，教唆</td><td>at his instigation<br>在他的教唆下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike began at the instigation of two union leaders.  罢工是在两位工会领袖的煽动下开始的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motivation</td><td>/ˌmoʊtəˈveɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>动机，动力</td><td>lack of motivation<br>缺乏动力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor pay explains the team's total lack of motivation.  报酬低解释了团队完全缺乏动力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spur</td><td>/spɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激物</td><td>a spur to growth<br>增长的刺激因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising exports acted as a powerful spur to industrial growth.  出口增长成为工业发展的强大刺激因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulus</td><td>/ˈstɪmjələs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>刺激物，促进因素</td><td>an economic stimulus<br>经济刺激措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap loans acted as a stimulus to small businesses.  低息贷款成为小企业的刺激因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deterrent</td><td>/dɪˈtɜːrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>遏制因素，威慑物</td><td>a strong deterrent<br>强有力的威慑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy fines act as a deterrent to careless drivers.  重罚对粗心司机起到威慑作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mongrel</b></td><td>/ˈmʌŋɡrəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>杂种的；杂种狗</td><td>a cheerful mongrel dog<br>快乐的杂种狗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cheerful mongrel dog followed us along the beach for nearly a mile.  一只快乐的杂种狗沿着海滩跟了我们将近一英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crossbred</td><td>/ˈkrɔsˌbrɛd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杂交的，杂种的</td><td>a crossbred calf<br>杂交的小牛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crossbred sheep survive the mountain winters much better.  杂交的绵羊更能耐住山区的冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hybrid</td><td>/ˈhaɪbrɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>杂交品种，混合品种</td><td>a hardy hybrid<br>耐寒的杂交种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This rose is a hybrid bred for cold northern gardens.  这种玫瑰是为寒冷北方花园培育的杂交品种。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 purebred</td><td>/ˈpjʊrbred/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>纯种的，纯血统的</td><td>a purebred horse<br>纯种的马</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her purebred collie won every prize at the county show.  她的纯种柯利犬在县展上赢得所有奖项。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>monochromatic</b></td><td>/ˌmɑːnəkroʊˈmætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单色的</td><td>a monochromatic palette<br>单色的配色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The designer chose a monochromatic scheme of soft grays.  设计师选了柔灰的单色方案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arid</td><td>/ˈærɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>枯燥无趣的；干燥的</td><td>an arid lecture<br>枯燥的讲座</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor's arid lecture emptied half the seats before the break.  这位教授枯燥乏味的讲座在课间前就空了一半座位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dreary</td><td>/ˈdrɪri/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调乏味的</td><td>a dreary afternoon<br>沉闷的下午</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We spent a dreary afternoon indoors watching rain streak the windows.  我们在室内度过一个沉闷的下午，看着雨水划过窗玻璃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 drudging</del></td><td>/ˈdrʌdʒɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>苦干的，单调乏味的</td><td>drudging office work<br>枯燥的办公室工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of drudging clerical work dulled his early ambition.  多年枯燥的文书苦工磨钝了他早年的雄心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dull</td><td>/dʌl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>迟钝的；乏味的</td><td>a dull student<br>迟钝的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecture was so dull that half the class slept.  讲座乏味得让一半学生睡着了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jejune</td><td>/dʒɪˈdʒuːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无趣乏味的</td><td>a jejune argument<br>乏味浅薄的论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics dismissed the sequel as jejune and repetitive.  评论家认为这部续集乏味且重复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monotonous</td><td>/məˈnɑːtənəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，无变化的</td><td>a monotonous voice<br>单调的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His monotonous voice made the afternoon lecture almost impossible to sit through.  他单调的声音让下午的讲座几乎难以听完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pedestrian</td><td>/pəˈdestriən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平庸无奇的，令人厌倦的</td><td>a pedestrian style<br>平庸的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reviewer called the plot pedestrian and the dialogue painfully flat.  评论者称其情节平庸，对话乏味得令人难受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ponderous</td><td>/ˈpɑːndərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉重的</td><td>ponderous machinery<br>笨重的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ponderous machinery filled the workshop and shook the floor all day.  笨重的机器占满车间，整天震动地板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stale</td><td>/steɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧乏味的</td><td>stale jokes<br>老掉牙的笑话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His stale jokes made the audience shift restlessly in their seats.  他老掉牙的笑话让观众在座位上不安地挪动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stodgy</td><td>/ˈstɑːdʒi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古板守旧的，乏味的</td><td>a stodgy old textbook<br>一本乏味的旧教材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The course relied on a stodgy old textbook nobody enjoyed reading.  这门课用的是一本没人爱读的乏味旧教材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tiresome</td><td>/ˈtaɪərsəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌烦的</td><td>a tiresome chore<br>烦人的杂事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Washing dishes by hand is a tiresome nightly chore.  手洗碗碟是每晚烦人的活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absorbing</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrbɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，吸引人的</td><td>an absorbing novel<br>引人入胜的小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I found her account of the voyage utterly absorbing.  我觉得她对航行的叙述极其引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engaging</td><td>/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有吸引力的，讨人喜欢的</td><td>an engaging personality<br>讨人喜欢的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His engaging manner made strangers feel comfortable within minutes.  他讨人喜欢的举止让陌生人几分钟内就放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engrossing</td><td>/ɪnˈɡroʊsɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，令人专注的</td><td>an engrossing story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary was so engrossing that nobody left early.  这部纪录片如此引人入胜，没人提早离场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gripping</td><td>/ˈɡrɪpɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>扣人心弦的，紧张的</td><td>a gripping thriller<br>扣人心弦的惊悚片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final chapter is gripping from start to finish.  最后一章从头到尾都扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interesting</td><td>/ˈɪntrəstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有趣的，引人入胜的</td><td>an interesting proposal<br>一个有趣的提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an interesting objection nobody had anticipated.  她提出了一个无人预料到的有趣反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intriguing</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtriːɡɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人好奇的，有趣的</td><td>an intriguing question<br>引人好奇的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an intriguing point that nobody had considered.  她提出了一个无人考虑过的有趣观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 involving</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，扣人心弦的</td><td>an involving story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary is more involving than any thriller this year.  这部纪录片比今年任何惊悚片都更扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 riveting</td><td>/ˈrɪvɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吸引人的，极迷人的</td><td>a riveting account<br>引人入胜的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary offers a riveting account of life inside the mountain villages.  这部纪录片对山村生活的叙述引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>monologue</b></td><td>/ˈmɑːnəlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>独白</td><td>a long monologue<br>长篇独白</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He delivered a long monologue about his childhood while everyone waited politely.  他讲了一大段关于童年的独白，众人礼貌地等着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soliloquy</td><td>/səˈlɪləkwi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>（尤指自言自语的）独白</td><td>Hamlet's famous soliloquy<br>哈姆雷特的著名独白</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hamlet's famous soliloquy reveals his deepest doubts about life and death.  哈姆雷特的著名独白揭示了他对生死的深切疑问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solo</td><td>/ˈsoʊˌloʊ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>独奏，独唱；单独的</td><td>a violin solo<br>小提琴独奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She played a short violin solo before the interval.  她在中场休息前演奏了一段小提琴独奏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 speech</td><td>/ˈspiːtʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>演讲；言语</td><td>deliver a speech<br>发表演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He delivered a short speech thanking all the volunteers.  他发表简短演讲感谢所有志愿者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dialogue</td><td>/ˈdaɪəˌlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对话，对白</td><td>open a dialogue<br>开启对话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides finally opened a dialogue about wages.  双方终于就工资开启对话。</td></tr>
</tbody>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stodgy</td><td>/ˈstɑːdʒi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古板守旧的，乏味的</td><td>a stodgy old textbook<br>一本乏味的旧教材</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The course relied on a stodgy old textbook nobody enjoyed reading.  这门课用的是一本没人爱读的乏味旧教材。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tiresome</td><td>/ˈtaɪərsəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人厌烦的</td><td>a tiresome chore<br>烦人的杂事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Washing dishes by hand is a tiresome nightly chore.  手洗碗碟是每晚烦人的活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 absorbing</td><td>/əbˈzɔːrbɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，吸引人的</td><td>an absorbing novel<br>引人入胜的小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I found her account of the voyage utterly absorbing.  我觉得她对航行的叙述极其引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engaging</td><td>/ɪnˈɡeɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有吸引力的，讨人喜欢的</td><td>an engaging personality<br>讨人喜欢的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His engaging manner made strangers feel comfortable within minutes.  他讨人喜欢的举止让陌生人几分钟内就放松下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 engrossing</td><td>/ɪnˈɡroʊsɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，令人专注的</td><td>an engrossing story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary was so engrossing that nobody left early.  这部纪录片如此引人入胜，没人提早离场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gripping</td><td>/ˈɡrɪpɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>扣人心弦的，紧张的</td><td>a gripping thriller<br>扣人心弦的惊悚片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final chapter is gripping from start to finish.  最后一章从头到尾都扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 interesting</td><td>/ˈɪntrəstɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有趣的，引人入胜的</td><td>an interesting proposal<br>一个有趣的提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an interesting objection nobody had anticipated.  她提出了一个无人预料到的有趣反对意见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intriguing</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtriːɡɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人好奇的，有趣的</td><td>an intriguing question<br>引人好奇的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She raised an intriguing point that nobody had considered.  她提出了一个无人考虑过的有趣观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 involving</td><td>/ɪnˈvɑːlvɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>引人入胜的，扣人心弦的</td><td>an involving story<br>引人入胜的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary is more involving than any thriller this year.  这部纪录片比今年任何惊悚片都更扣人心弦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 riveting</td><td>/ˈrɪvɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吸引人的，极迷人的</td><td>a riveting account<br>引人入胜的叙述</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The documentary offers a riveting account of life inside the mountain villages.  这部纪录片对山村生活的叙述引人入胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>montage</b></td><td>/mɑːnˈtɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩</td><td>a montage of images<br>图像的拼贴组合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhibit opened with a montage of images from a century of protest.  展览以一组来自百年抗争的图像拼贴开场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agglomerate</td><td>/əˈɡlɑːməreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结块，聚成一团</td><td>agglomerate into lumps<br>聚结成块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wet powder agglomerated into hard grey lumps.  湿粉结成了灰色的硬块。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collage</td><td>/kəˈlɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拼贴画，混合物</td><td>a collage of photographs<br>照片拼贴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her film is a collage of interviews and old home movies.  她的影片是访谈与旧家庭录像的拼贴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hodgepodge</td><td>/ˈhɑːdʒpɑːdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩</td><td>a hodgepodge of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The house is a hodgepodge of every style he admired.  这房子是他欣赏过的各种风格的大杂烩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 medley</td><td>/ˈmedli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩，混合物</td><td>a medley of flavors<br>各种风味的混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The buffet table offered a medley of flavors from six different countries.  自助餐台上供应着来自六个不同国家的各式风味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motley</td><td>/ˈmɑːtli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混杂的，富于变化的</td><td>a motley crowd<br>混杂的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A motley crowd of farmers, students, and shopkeepers filled the town square.  农民、学生和店主组成的混杂人群挤满了市镇广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salad</td><td>/ˈsæləd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩，混合物；色拉</td><td>a salad of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The album is a salad of jazz, rock and folk tunes.  这张专辑是爵士、摇滚和民谣的大杂烩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variety</td><td>/vəˈraɪəti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样，种类繁多</td><td>a wide variety of dishes<br>各式各样的菜肴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offers a wide variety of local cheeses.  市场上有各式各样的本地奶酪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>moratorium</b></td><td>/ˌmɔːrəˈtɔːriəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>暂停，延期执行</td><td>a moratorium on hunting<br>暂停狩猎的禁令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council imposed a moratorium on new building permits.  议会暂停发放新的建筑许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abeyance</td><td>/əˈbeɪəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>中止，搁置</td><td>hold a plan in abeyance<br>暂时搁置计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project was held in abeyance until funding arrived.  项目被搁置，直到资金到位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delay</td><td>/dɪˈleɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>延迟，推迟</td><td>delay the departure<br>推迟出发</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy fog delayed the flight by more than four hours.  大雾使航班延误了四个多小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doldrums</td><td>/ˈdoʊldrəmz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>低迷，停滞</td><td>in the doldrums<br>处于低迷状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The housing market has been in the doldrums all year.  房地产市场整年都处于低迷状态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dormancy</td><td>/ˈdɔːrmənsi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>休眠，静止状态</td><td>break dormancy<br>打破休眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Seeds emerge from dormancy when the soil warms.  土壤变暖时种子便结束休眠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 latency</td><td>/ˈleɪtənsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>潜伏，潜在状态；延迟</td><td>a period of latency<br>一段潜伏期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus can remain in latency for many years.  这种病毒可以潜伏多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quiescence</td><td>/kwiˈesəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>静止，休止状态</td><td>a period of quiescence<br>一段静止期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano entered a long period of quiescence.  这座火山进入了长期的静止期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 postponement</td><td>/poʊstˈpoʊnmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>延期，推迟</td><td>postponement of the trial<br>审判延期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The postponement of the trial angered the victim's family.  审判延期激怒了受害者的家人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suspension</td><td>/səˈspenʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>暂停，中止</td><td>a temporary suspension<br>暂时中止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike led to a suspension of all train services.  罢工导致所有列车服务暂停。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 resumption</td><td>/rɪˈzʌmpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恢复，重新开始</td><td>the resumption of talks<br>谈判的恢复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone welcomed the resumption of the peace talks.  各方都欢迎和谈的恢复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>morbid</b></td><td>/ˈmɔːrbɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>病态的，阴郁不健康的</td><td>a morbid fascination<br>病态的迷恋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She developed a morbid fascination with old newspaper reports of unsolved crimes.  她对旧报纸上未解案件的报道产生了病态的迷恋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diseased</td><td>/dɪˈziːzd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>患病的，有病的</td><td>a diseased liver<br>患病的肝脏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon removed the diseased tissue from the lung.  外科医生切除了肺部的病变组织。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pathological</td><td>/ˌpæθəˈlɑːdʒɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>病态的；病理的</td><td>a pathological liar<br>病态的说谎者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pathological fear of failure kept him from trying anything new.  他对失败的病态恐惧使他不敢尝试任何新事物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 brainsick</del></td><td>/ˈbreɪnsɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神失常的，疯狂的</td><td>a brainsick scheme<br>疯狂的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a brainsick general would attack in such weather.  只有疯狂的将军才会在这种天气进攻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 crazy</td><td>/ˈkreɪzi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疯狂的，荒唐的</td><td>a crazy idea<br>疯狂的主意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It sounded like a crazy idea until it actually worked.  这主意听着很疯，直到它真的成功了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deranged</td><td>/dɪˈreɪndʒd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精神错乱的</td><td>a deranged mind<br>错乱的头脑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The letters were clearly written by a deranged admirer.  这些信显然出自一个精神错乱的仰慕者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lunatic</td><td>/ˈluːnəˌtɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>疯狂的；疯子</td><td>a lunatic plan<br>疯狂的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Driving at that speed in fog is simply lunatic.  在雾中以那种速度开车简直疯狂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 psychotic</td><td>/saɪˈkɑːtɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>精神病的；精神病人</td><td>a psychotic episode<br>一次精神病发作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was hospitalized after a brief psychotic episode.  他在一次短暂的精神病发作后住院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 verdant</td><td>/ˈvɜːrdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（因长满植物而）翠绿的，郁郁葱葱的</td><td>verdant hills<br>翠绿的山丘</td></tr>
