<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the rains the verdant valley filled with wildflowers.  雨后，翠绿的山谷开满了野花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hale</td><td>/heɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>强壮的，健壮的</td><td>hale and hearty<br>身体强健，精神饱满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At eighty he remains hale enough to walk five miles a day.  八十岁的他依然强健，每天能走五英里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 salubrious</td><td>/səˈluːbriəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益健康的</td><td>a salubrious mountain climate<br>有益健康的山地气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors sent him to a salubrious mountain village to recover his strength.  医生把他送到有益健康的山村去恢复体力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wholesome</td><td>/ˈhoʊlsəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有益身心健康的</td><td>wholesome home-cooked food<br>有益健康的家常饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children grew strong on wholesome food and plenty of fresh air.  孩子们靠有益健康的食物和充足的新鲜空气长得很结实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mordant</b></td><td>/ˈmɔːrdnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖酸刻薄的</td><td>mordant wit<br>尖刻的机智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mordant wit amused readers but cost him several close friendships.  他尖刻的机智取悦了读者，却让他失去几段亲密友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acerbic</td><td>/əˈsɜːrbɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（语调）尖酸的，刻薄的</td><td>acerbic wit<br>尖刻的机智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reviewer is famous for her acerbic comments on new plays.  这位评论家以对新剧的尖酸评论闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acid</td><td>/ˈæsəd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>尖刻的；酸的；酸</td><td>an acid remark<br>一句尖刻的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her acid comment about his writing silenced the whole table.  她对他文章的尖刻评论让全桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acrid</td><td>/ˈækrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刻薄的；辛辣刺鼻的</td><td>acrid smoke<br>刺鼻的烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An acrid smell of burning plastic filled the hallway.  一股塑料烧焦的刺鼻气味充满了走廊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 barbed</td><td>/ˈbɑːrbd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；有倒刺的</td><td>a barbed comment<br>带刺的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her barbed compliment made the whole table fall silent.  她那句带刺的赞美让全桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 caustic</td><td>/ˈkɔːstɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>挖苦讽刺的，刻薄的</td><td>a caustic remark<br>刻薄的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His caustic remarks about the film upset the young director.  他对影片刻薄的评论让年轻导演很难过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 corrosive</td><td>/kəˈroʊsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>腐蚀性的；讽刺性的</td><td>a corrosive chemical<br>腐蚀性化学品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Salt water is highly corrosive and slowly eats through the ship's hull.  盐水腐蚀性很强，会慢慢蚀穿船体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pungent</td><td>/ˈpʌndʒənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言辞）辛辣尖刻的；刺鼻的</td><td>pungent criticism<br>辛辣的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pungent criticism of the report left everyone at the table silent.  他对这份报告辛辣的批评让全桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sardonic</td><td>/sɑːrˈdɑːnɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>讥讽的，嘲笑的</td><td>a sardonic smile<br>讥讽的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered with a sardonic laugh and said nothing more.  他讥讽地笑了一声，什么也没再说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 satiric</td><td>/səˈtɪrɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>讽刺的，讥讽的</td><td>a satiric novel<br>一部讽刺小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine published a satiric cartoon about the election.  这家杂志刊登了一幅关于选举的讽刺漫画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scalding</td><td>/ˈskɔːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>滚烫的；（言辞）严厉的</td><td>scalding criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic wrote a scalding review of the opening night.  评论家为首演之夜写了一篇严厉的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scathing</td><td>/ˈskeɪðɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖酸刻薄的，严厉的</td><td>a scathing attack<br>尖刻的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a scathing remark about his sloppy research.  她对他草率的研究作了一句尖刻的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tart</td><td>/ˈtɑːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖刻的；酸的</td><td>a tart reply<br>尖刻的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her tart reply silenced everyone at the dinner table.  她尖刻的回答让餐桌上所有人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 congenial</td><td>/kənˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>和善的，友好的</td><td>a congenial host<br>一位和善的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our congenial host made every guest feel completely at home.  我们和善的主人让每位客人都感到自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 genial</td><td>/ˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼亲切的；温和宜人的</td><td>a genial host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our genial neighbor invited the whole street over for dinner.  我们和蔼可亲的邻居请整条街的人来吃晚饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>moribund</b></td><td>/ˈmɔːrɪbʌnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>垂死的，即将消亡的</td><td>a moribund industry<br>垂死的行业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap imports left the region's textile industry moribund within a single decade.  廉价进口品在十年内就让该地区的纺织业濒于消亡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antiquated</td><td>/ˈæntɪkweɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老的，过时的</td><td>an antiquated system<br>过时的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The office still runs on an antiquated system built in the eighties.  办公室仍在使用八十年代建成的过时系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 archaic</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，久远的</td><td>archaic legal language<br>古旧的法律用语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract still uses archaic terms that nobody understands.  这份合同仍在使用无人理解的古旧术语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dated</td><td>/ˈdeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的</td><td>a dated hairstyle<br>过时的发型</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The furniture looked hopelessly dated beside the building's new glass walls.  在大楼新的玻璃幕墙旁，这些家具显得过时至极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fossilized</td><td>/ˈfɑːsəˌlaɪzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵化的，化石化的</td><td>fossilized attitudes<br>僵化的观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The department's fossilized rules discouraged every new hire.  部门僵化的规定让每个新人失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moth-eaten</td><td>/ˈmɔːθ ˌiːtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虫蛀的；陈旧过时的</td><td>a moth-eaten coat<br>被虫蛀的外套</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He arrived in a moth-eaten sweater from his college days.  他穿着大学时代那件被虫蛀的毛衣来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outdated</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌdeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，陈旧的</td><td>outdated equipment<br>过时的设备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their outdated software could not handle the new files.  他们过时的软件无法处理新文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 outworn</del></td><td>/ˌaʊtˈwɔrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧的，过时的</td><td>outworn traditions<br>过时的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quietly abandoned the outworn methods of her teachers.  她悄悄抛弃了老师们过时的方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rusty</td><td>/ˈrʌsti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音生涩沙哑的</td><td>a rusty voice<br>生涩的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of silence his voice sounded rusty and thin.  多年不语后，他的声音听起来生涩而单薄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fresh</td><td>/ˈfreʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新颖的；新鲜的</td><td>a fresh approach<br>新颖的方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team needs a fresh approach to this old problem.  团队需要用新颖的方法处理这个老问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 new</td><td>/ˈnuː/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新的，最近出现的</td><td>a brand new car<br>一辆全新的汽车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moved into a new apartment near the river.  他们搬进了河边一套新公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>morose</b></td><td>/məˈroʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>grow morose and silent<br>变得忧郁沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After losing the election he grew morose and rarely left his apartment.  落选之后他变得忧郁，很少离开公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bleak</td><td>/ˈbliːk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（天气）阴冷的；荒凉的</td><td>a bleak winter day<br>阴冷的冬日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The moor looked bleak under grey November skies.  灰暗的十一月天空下，荒原显得阴冷荒凉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chill</td><td>/ˈtʃɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使胆寒，使沮丧；寒冷</td><td>chill his enthusiasm<br>打消他的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The news of the layoffs chilled everyone in the office.  裁员的消息让办公室里的每个人都心生寒意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dark</td><td>/ˈdɑːrk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴郁的；黑暗的</td><td>a dark mood<br>阴郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He fell into a dark mood after reading the letter.  读了那封信后他陷入阴郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 depressed</td><td>/dɪˈprest/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不开心的，情绪不高的，消沉的</td><td>feel deeply depressed<br>感到十分消沉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt depressed for weeks after the letter of rejection arrived.  收到拒信之后，她消沉了好几个星期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dire</td><td>/ˈdaɪər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的，恐怖的；迫切的</td><td>dire consequences<br>可怕的后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report warned of dire consequences if the dam were not repaired soon.  报告警告说，若不尽快修复大坝，后果将十分可怕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dour</td><td>/ˈdaʊər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严厉的</td><td>a dour expression<br>严厉的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guard's dour expression discouraged anyone from asking him for directions.  警卫严厉的表情让人不敢向他问路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gloomy</td><td>/ˈɡluːmi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的</td><td>a gloomy mood<br>忧郁的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew gloomy whenever the winter rain lasted more than a week.  每当冬雨连下一周以上，她就变得忧郁起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lugubrious</td><td>/ləˈɡuːbriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（故作夸张的）悲哀的</td><td>a lugubrious expression<br>夸张悲哀的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a lugubrious expression whenever anyone mentioned his ruined garden.  每次有人提到他毁掉的花园，他就露出夸张悲哀的表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saturnine</td><td>/ˈsætərnaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧郁的，阴沉的</td><td>a saturnine temperament<br>阴沉的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His saturnine expression discouraged anyone from starting a cheerful conversation.  他阴沉的表情让人不敢开启轻松的话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solemn</td><td>/ˈsɑːləm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>庄严的，郑重的</td><td>a solemn promise<br>郑重的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ceremony was solemn and lasted almost two hours.  仪式庄严肃穆，持续了近两个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sulky</td><td>/ˈsʌlki/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>赌气的，生气不说话的</td><td>a sulky teenager<br>赌气的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sulky child refused to speak for the whole long afternoon.  那个赌气的孩子整个下午都不肯说话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sullen</td><td>/ˈsʌlən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>闷闷不乐的</td><td>a sullen silence<br>闷闷不乐的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sullen silence fell over the table when the topic was raised.  提到这个话题时，餐桌上陷入闷闷不乐的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surly</td><td>/ˈsɜːrli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气不好的</td><td>a surly waiter<br>一个态度恶劣的侍者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A surly waiter took our order without once looking up.  一个态度恶劣的侍者点单时始终没抬头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bright</td><td>/ˈbraɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快活的，开朗的；明亮的</td><td>a bright smile<br>开朗的笑容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She greeted every customer with a bright and genuine smile.  她用开朗真诚的笑容迎接每位顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheerful</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，愉快的</td><td>a cheerful greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed cheerful even after the long delay.  即使长时间延误后他仍保持愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 festive</td><td>/ˈfestɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢乐的，节日的</td><td>a festive mood<br>欢乐的气氛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole street took on a festive air by evening.  到傍晚整条街都染上了节日气息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jovial</td><td>/ˈdʒoʊviəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愉快的</td><td>a jovial host<br>愉快的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our jovial host kept the guests laughing throughout the long winter evening.  我们愉快的主人让客人们在漫长的冬夜里笑声不断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 jocund</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɑːkənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢快的，高兴的</td><td>a jocund gathering<br>欢快的聚会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jocund crowd sang and danced until the last lantern went out.  欢快的人群唱歌跳舞，直到最后一盏灯笼熄灭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sanguine</td><td>/ˈsæŋɡwɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的，确信的</td><td>sanguine about the future<br>对未来乐观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains sanguine about the harvest despite three weeks without rain.  尽管三周无雨，她对收成依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lighthearted</td><td>/ˌlaɪtˈhɑːrtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻松愉快的，无忧无虑的</td><td>a lighthearted comedy<br>轻松的喜剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We wanted a lighthearted film after such a stressful week.  在如此紧张的一周后，我们想看部轻松的电影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rejoiced</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪst/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>欢欣，喜悦（rejoice 的过去式）</td><td>rejoiced at the news<br>为这消息而欢欣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced when the missing boy finally came home.  失踪的男孩终于回家时，全村都欢欣不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mosaic</b></td><td>/moʊˈzeɪɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>综合物，马赛克般的东西</td><td>a mosaic of cultures<br>多元文化的拼合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighborhood is a mosaic of cultures, languages, and cooking smells.  这个街区是文化、语言与烹饪气味的拼合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agglomerate</td><td>/əˈɡlɑːməreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>结块，聚成一团</td><td>agglomerate into lumps<br>聚结成块</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The wet powder agglomerated into hard grey lumps.  湿粉结成了灰色的硬块。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 collage</td><td>/kəˈlɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>拼贴画，混合物</td><td>a collage of photographs<br>照片拼贴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her film is a collage of interviews and old home movies.  她的影片是访谈与旧家庭录像的拼贴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hodgepodge</td><td>/ˈhɑːdʒpɑːdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩</td><td>a hodgepodge of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The house is a hodgepodge of every style he admired.  这房子是他欣赏过的各种风格的大杂烩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jumble</td><td>/ˈdʒʌmbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>混乱，杂乱堆积；使混乱</td><td>a jumble of ideas<br>杂乱的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her drawer held a jumble of receipts, coins, and old keys.  她的抽屉里杂乱地堆着收据、硬币和旧钥匙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 montage</td><td>/mɑːnˈtɑːʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩</td><td>a montage of images<br>图像的拼贴组合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhibit opened with a montage of images from a century of protest.  展览以一组来自百年抗争的图像拼贴开场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 motley</td><td>/ˈmɑːtli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混杂的，富于变化的</td><td>a motley crowd<br>混杂的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A motley crowd of farmers, students, and shopkeepers filled the town square.  农民、学生和店主组成的混杂人群挤满了市镇广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muddle</td><td>/ˈmʌdəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使混乱，弄糟；混乱</td><td>muddle the facts<br>把事实弄乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many footnotes muddled the argument of his short essay.  过多脚注把他短文的论证弄乱了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 salad</td><td>/ˈsæləd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大杂烩，混合物；色拉</td><td>a salad of styles<br>风格的大杂烩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The album is a salad of jazz, rock and folk tunes.  这张专辑是爵士、摇滚和民谣的大杂烩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shuffle</td><td>/ˈʃʌfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>混杂，打乱；洗牌</td><td>a shuffle of old photographs<br>一堆打乱的旧照片</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He shuffled the papers into a hopeless mixture.  他把文件混成了一团乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variety</td><td>/vəˈraɪəti/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>多样，种类繁多</td><td>a wide variety of dishes<br>各式各样的菜肴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offers a wide variety of local cheeses.  市场上有各式各样的本地奶酪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 welter</td><td>/ˈweltər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>混乱，动乱</td><td>a welter of details<br>杂乱的一堆细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report buried its main point in a welter of small details.  这份报告把要点埋在了杂乱的琐碎细节之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>monolithic</b></td><td>/ˌmɑːnəˈlɪθɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>铁板一块的，僵化统一的</td><td>a monolithic organization<br>铁板一块的组织</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party was less monolithic than outsiders assumed, and quiet disagreements ran deep.  这个政党并不像外界以为的那样铁板一块，暗中的分歧很深。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 homogeneous</td><td>/ˌhoʊməˈdʒiːniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同质的，同类的</td><td>a homogeneous population<br>同质的人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The town's population is unusually homogeneous in language and faith.  该镇人口在语言和信仰上异常同质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uniform</td><td>/ˈjuːnəˌfɔːrm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>一致的，统一的</td><td>a uniform standard<br>统一的标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bricks were baked to a uniform shade of red.  这些砖被烧成统一的红色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alike</td><td>/əˈlaɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>相似的，同样地</td><td>look very much alike<br>看起来很相像</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two paintings look alike until you study the brushwork.  这两幅画看起来相像，直到你细看笔法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 identical</td><td>/aɪˈdentɪkəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全相同的，同一的</td><td>virtually identical results<br>几乎完全相同的结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two samples produced identical readings on every machine.  两个样本在每台机器上都得出相同读数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 motley</td><td>/ˈmɑːtli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混杂的，富于变化的</td><td>a motley crowd<br>混杂的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A motley crowd of farmers, students, and shopkeepers filled the town square.  农民、学生和店主组成的混杂人群挤满了市镇广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assorted</td><td>/əˈsɔːrtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>各式各样的，混合的</td><td>assorted chocolates<br>什锦巧克力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She brought a box of assorted chocolates to the office.  她带了一盒什锦巧克力到办公室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 eclectic</td><td>/ɪˈklektɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>博采众长的；不拘一格的</td><td>an eclectic collection<br>混合多元的收藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her taste in music is remarkably eclectic and hard to label.  她的音乐品味相当博杂，难以归类。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heterogeneous</td><td>/ˌhetərəˈdʒiːniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>异质的，混杂的</td><td>a heterogeneous mixture<br>混杂的混合物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The class is a heterogeneous mixture of ages and backgrounds.  这个班是年龄和背景的混杂组合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mixed</td><td>/ˈmɪkst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混合的，掺杂的；好坏交织的</td><td>mixed feelings<br>复杂的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I have mixed feelings about moving abroad.  对于出国我心情复杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patchwork</td><td>/ˈpætʃwɜːrk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>拼凑物；拼凑而成的</td><td>a patchwork of fields<br>拼块般的田野</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the plane the valley looked like a patchwork of green fields.  从飞机上看，山谷像是一块块绿色田地拼成的图案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 piebald</td><td>/ˈpaɪbɔːld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有黑白斑块的</td><td>a piebald horse<br>黑白花的马</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A piebald horse grazed quietly beside the wooden fence.  一匹黑白花的马在木栅栏旁静静吃草。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 varied</td><td>/ˈverid/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>各式各样的，多变的</td><td>a varied diet<br>多样的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A varied diet keeps both cook and eater interested.  多样的饮食让做饭的和吃饭的都有兴致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>motheaten</b></del></td><td>/ˈmɔːθˌiːtən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虫蛀的；陈旧过时的</td><td>motheaten ideas<br>陈腐的观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His motheaten ideas about education sounded strange to the younger teachers.  他陈腐过时的教育观念在年轻教师听来很奇怪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antiquated</td><td>/ˈæntɪkweɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>古老的，过时的</td><td>an antiquated system<br>过时的系统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The office still runs on an antiquated system built in the eighties.  办公室仍在使用八十年代建成的过时系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 archaic</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，久远的</td><td>archaic legal language<br>古旧的法律用语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract still uses archaic terms that nobody understands.  这份合同仍在使用无人理解的古旧术语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dated</td><td>/ˈdeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的</td><td>a dated hairstyle<br>过时的发型</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The furniture looked hopelessly dated beside the building's new glass walls.  在大楼新的玻璃幕墙旁，这些家具显得过时至极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fossilized</td><td>/ˈfɑːsəˌlaɪzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>僵化的，化石化的</td><td>fossilized attitudes<br>僵化的观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The department's fossilized rules discouraged every new hire.  部门僵化的规定让每个新人失望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moribund</td><td>/ˈmɔːrɪbʌnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>垂死的，即将消亡的</td><td>a moribund industry<br>垂死的行业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap imports left the region's textile industry moribund within a single decade.  廉价进口品在十年内就让该地区的纺织业濒于消亡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outdated</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌdeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过时的，陈旧的</td><td>outdated equipment<br>过时的设备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their outdated software could not handle the new files.  他们过时的软件无法处理新文件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 outworn</del></td><td>/ˌaʊtˈwɔrn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>陈旧的，过时的</td><td>outworn traditions<br>过时的传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She quietly abandoned the outworn methods of her teachers.  她悄悄抛弃了老师们过时的方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rusty</td><td>/ˈrʌsti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>声音生涩沙哑的</td><td>a rusty voice<br>生涩的嗓音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After years of silence his voice sounded rusty and thin.  多年不语后，他的声音听起来生涩而单薄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fresh</td><td>/ˈfreʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新颖的；新鲜的</td><td>a fresh approach<br>新颖的方法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team needs a fresh approach to this old problem.  团队需要用新颖的方法处理这个老问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 new</td><td>/ˈnuː/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>新的，最近出现的</td><td>a brand new car<br>一辆全新的汽车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moved into a new apartment near the river.  他们搬进了河边一套新公寓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>motile</b></td><td>/ˈmoʊtɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>能动的</td><td>motile bacteria<br>能运动的细菌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Motile bacteria swam rapidly across the slide under the student's microscope.  能动的细菌在学生显微镜下的载玻片上快速游动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mobile</td><td>/ˈmoʊbəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>流动的，可移动的</td><td>a mobile population<br>流动人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young workers today are far more mobile than their parents.  如今的年轻工人比父辈流动性强得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 movable</td><td>/ˈmuːvəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可移动的</td><td>movable partitions<br>可移动的隔板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hall is divided by movable partitions into three rooms.  大厅用可移动隔板分成三间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 portable</td><td>/ˈpɔːrtəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>便携的</td><td>a portable heater<br>便携取暖器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She carried a portable heater into the drafty studio.  她把便携取暖器搬进四处漏风的工作室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transportable</td><td>/trænˈspɔːrtəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可运输的，便于搬运的</td><td>easily transportable goods<br>易运输的货物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic uses transportable equipment for remote villages.  该诊所为偏远村庄使用可搬运设备。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 immobile</td><td>/ɪˈmoʊbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不动的，固定的</td><td>remain immobile<br>保持不动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The injured hiker lay immobile until help arrived.  受伤的徒步者一动不动地躺着直到救援到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>motivate</b></td><td>/ˈmoʊtɪveɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发</td><td>motivate the students<br>激励学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small weekly rewards motivate the students far better than long lectures do.  每周的小奖励比长篇说教更能激励学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 galvanize</td><td>/ˈɡælvənaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（好似用电击）刺激</td><td>galvanize the team into action<br>激励团队行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach's angry speech galvanized the team into immediate action.  教练愤怒的讲话激励球队立刻行动起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impel</td><td>/ɪmˈpel/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱使，激励</td><td>impel someone to act<br>驱使某人行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Curiosity impelled her to open the sealed envelope.  好奇心驱使她打开了密封的信封。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 innervate</td><td>/ˈɪnərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激；使有神经分布</td><td>innervate the muscle<br>为肌肉提供神经</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 These fibers innervate the muscles of the lower jaw.  这些纤维为下颌肌肉提供神经。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 provoke</td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rouse</td><td>/raʊz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；唤醒</td><td>rouse public anger<br>激起公众愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech was meant to rouse public anger against the proposed tax.  那场演讲意在激起公众对拟征税款的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulate</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发；促进</td><td>stimulate economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm rain stimulated the seeds to sprout within a week.  温暖的雨水刺激种子一周内发芽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discourage</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使气馁；劝阻</td><td>discourage him from trying<br>劝他不要尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early failure should not discourage a young researcher.  早期的失败不该让年轻研究者气馁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dishearten</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrtn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使沮丧，使失去信心</td><td>disheartened by the news<br>因这消息而沮丧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The string of early losses disheartened even the most loyal fans.  开局连输让最忠实的球迷也失去了信心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>motley</b></td><td>/ˈmɑːtli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混杂的，富于变化的</td><td>a motley crowd<br>混杂的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A motley crowd of farmers, students, and shopkeepers filled the town square.  农民、学生和店主组成的混杂人群挤满了市镇广场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assorted</td><td>/əˈsɔːrtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>各式各样的，混合的</td><td>assorted chocolates<br>什锦巧克力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She brought a box of assorted chocolates to the office.  她带了一盒什锦巧克力到办公室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chromatic</td><td>/krəˈmætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>彩色的</td><td>brilliant chromatic patterns<br>绚丽的彩色图案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The quilt showed brilliant chromatic patterns across every square.  这条被子每一格都是绚丽的彩色图案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kaleidoscopic</td><td>/kəˌlaɪdəˈskɑpɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>千变万化的；万花筒般的</td><td>a kaleidoscopic display<br>千变万化的景象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The festival offered a kaleidoscopic mix of music and costume.  节庆呈现出千变万化的音乐与服饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heterogeneous</td><td>/ˌhetərəˈdʒiːniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>异质的，混杂的</td><td>a heterogeneous mixture<br>混杂的混合物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The class is a heterogeneous mixture of ages and backgrounds.  这个班是年龄和背景的混杂组合。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indiscriminate</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈskrɪmɪnət/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不加区别的，随意的</td><td>indiscriminate shelling<br>无差别炮击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The indiscriminate use of pesticides harmed local bees.  杀虫剂的滥用伤害了当地蜜蜂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magpie</td><td>/ˈmæɡˌpaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>喜鹊；爱收集杂物者</td><td>a magpie collection<br>杂七杂八的收藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His study is a magpie hoard of maps, coins and shells.  他的书房是地图、硬币和贝壳的杂堆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 piebald</td><td>/ˈpaɪbɔːld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有黑白斑块的</td><td>a piebald horse<br>黑白花的马</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A piebald horse grazed quietly beside the wooden fence.  一匹黑白花的马在木栅栏旁静静吃草。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 variegated</td><td>/ˈverɪɡeɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杂色的，斑驳的</td><td>variegated leaves<br>斑驳的叶子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She filled the garden with variegated leaves of green and cream.  她在花园里种满绿白斑驳的叶子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 homogeneous</td><td>/ˌhoʊməˈdʒiːniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>同质的，同类的</td><td>a homogeneous population<br>同质的人口</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The town's population is unusually homogeneous in language and faith.  该镇人口在语言和信仰上异常同质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 monochromatic</td><td>/ˌmɑːnəkroʊˈmætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单色的</td><td>a monochromatic palette<br>单色的配色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The designer chose a monochromatic scheme of soft grays.  设计师选了柔灰的单色方案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 monotonous</td><td>/məˈnɑːtənəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，无变化的</td><td>a monotonous voice<br>单调的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His monotonous voice made the afternoon lecture almost impossible to sit through.  他单调的声音让下午的讲座几乎难以听完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unvaried</del></td><td>/ʌnˈvɛrid/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无变化的，单调的</td><td>an unvaried routine<br>毫无变化的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of unvaried routine left him restless and bored.  多年一成不变的日程让他烦躁厌倦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mottle</b></td><td>/ˈmɑːtl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使呈杂色，使有斑点</td><td>mottle the surface<br>使表面现斑点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of sun and rain had mottled the surface of the wooden door.  多年风吹日晒使木门表面布满了杂色斑点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blotch</td><td>/ˈblɑːtʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>斑点，污渍；使起斑</td><td>red blotches on skin<br>皮肤上的红斑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ink blotches covered the corner of the carefully written letter.  墨渍污了那封精心书写信件的一角。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dapple</td><td>/ˈdæpəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使有斑点，使斑驳</td><td>dapple the water<br>在水面洒下斑影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sunlight through the leaves dappled the path with gold.  阳光透过树叶把小路点成金色斑驳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dot</td><td>/ˈdɑːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使布满小点；小圆点</td><td>dot the map with pins<br>在地图上插满小旗</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small green islands dot the blue bay near the harbor.  小小的绿岛点缀在港口附近蔚蓝的海湾上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 marble</td><td>/ˈmɑːrbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使有大理石纹；大理石</td><td>marble the paper<br>给纸做出大理石纹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The binder marbled the endpapers in blue and cream.  装订师把封内页做成蓝白大理石纹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 splotch</td><td>/splɑtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>斑点，污渍；弄上斑点</td><td>a splotch of ink<br>一块墨渍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A splotch of ink spoiled the corner of the drawing.  一块墨渍毁了画的一角。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spot</td><td>/ˈspɑːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>污点，斑点；地点</td><td>a spot on the shirt<br>衬衫上的污点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A grease spot on the sleeve ruined his only white shirt.  袖子上的油污弄坏了他唯一的白衬衫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stain</td><td>/ˈsteɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>沾污，玷污；污点</td><td>stain his reputation<br>玷污他的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal stained the family name for two whole generations.  这桩丑闻让这个家族的名声蒙污两代人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blanch</td><td>/ˈblæntʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>使变白，漂白；发白</td><td>blanch the almonds<br>把杏仁焯白</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He blanched visibly when the bill was handed over.  账单递来时他明显脸色发白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>multifarious</b></td><td>/ˌmʌltɪˈferiəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多种多样的</td><td>multifarious duties<br>五花八门的职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her multifarious duties ranged from bookkeeping to repairing the office plumbing.  她五花八门的职责从记账到修办公室水管都有。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 divers</td><td>/ˈdaɪvərz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>各种的，若干的</td><td>for divers reasons<br>出于种种原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan failed for divers reasons never fully explained.  该计划因种种从未说清的原因失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 manifold</td><td>/ˈmænəˌfoʊld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>多方面的，多样的</td><td>manifold problems<br>多方面的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new director faced manifold problems in her first week.  新主任第一周就面对多方面的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 myriad</td><td>/ˈmɪriəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无限的，大量的</td><td>myriad possibilities<br>无数的可能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new technology opens myriad possibilities for teaching students in remote villages.  这项新技术为教偏远村庄的学生打开了无数可能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mournful</b></td><td>/ˈmɔːrnfl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲伤的</td><td>a mournful melody<br>悲伤的旋律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A mournful melody drifted from the church as the procession passed by.  送葬队伍经过时，教堂里飘出一段悲伤的旋律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aching</td><td>/ˈeɪkɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>痛苦的，酸痛的</td><td>an aching heart<br>痛苦的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wrote the letter with an aching heart.  她怀着痛苦的心写下那封信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 agonized</td><td>/ˈæɡəˌnaɪzd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>痛苦的，苦恼的</td><td>an agonized cry<br>痛苦的呼喊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an agonized look as the verdict was read.  宣读判决时她露出痛苦的神情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anguished</td><td>/ˈæŋɡwɪʃt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>极度痛苦的</td><td>an anguished cry<br>痛苦的哭喊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An anguished cry came from the far end of the corridor.  走廊尽头传来一声痛苦的哭喊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doleful</td><td>/ˈdoʊlfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲哀的，忧郁的</td><td>a doleful voice<br>悲哀的语调</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She told the whole story in a low doleful voice.  她用低沉悲哀的语调讲完了整件事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dolorous</td><td>/ˈdoʊlərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>忧伤的</td><td>a dolorous sigh<br>忧伤的叹息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A dolorous sigh escaped him whenever the subject of his hometown arose.  每次提到故乡，他都发出忧伤的叹息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grievous</td><td>/ˈɡriːvəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>严重的，令人痛苦的</td><td>a grievous mistake<br>严重的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report described grievous failures in patient care.  报告描述了病人护理中的严重失误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lamentable</td><td>/ləˈmentəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惋惜的，可悲的</td><td>a lamentable failure<br>令人痛惜的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The condition of the old school building is truly lamentable.  这所旧校舍的状况实在令人惋惜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lugubrious</td><td>/ləˈɡuːbriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（故作夸张的）悲哀的</td><td>a lugubrious expression<br>夸张悲哀的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore a lugubrious expression whenever anyone mentioned his ruined garden.  每次有人提到他毁掉的花园，他就露出夸张悲哀的表情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 melancholy</td><td>/ˈmelənkɑːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>忧郁，伤感</td><td>a mood of quiet melancholy<br>淡淡的忧郁情绪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Autumn rain always fills him with a vague melancholy.  秋雨总让他心里泛起一种莫名的忧郁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sad</td><td>/ˈsæd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲伤的，令人难过的</td><td>a sad story<br>悲伤的故事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is a sad story, but the ending offers hope.  这是个悲伤的故事，但结尾给人希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 woeful</td><td>/ˈwoʊfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲惨的，糟糕的</td><td>a woeful performance<br>糟糕的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team's woeful defending cost them the championship again.  球队糟糕的防守又一次让他们丢了冠军。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cheerful</td><td>/ˈtʃɪrfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>快乐的，愉快的</td><td>a cheerful greeting<br>愉快的问候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed cheerful even after the long delay.  即使长时间延误后他仍保持愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delighted</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪtəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高兴的，欣喜的</td><td>delighted with the gift<br>对礼物很欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was delighted to hear about her friend's promotion.  听说朋友升职，她非常高兴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 jocund</del></td><td>/ˈdʒɑːkənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>欢快的，高兴的</td><td>a jocund gathering<br>欢快的聚会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jocund crowd sang and danced until the last lantern went out.  欢快的人群唱歌跳舞，直到最后一盏灯笼熄灭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jovial</td><td>/ˈdʒoʊviəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愉快的</td><td>a jovial host<br>愉快的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our jovial host kept the guests laughing throughout the long winter evening.  我们愉快的主人让客人们在漫长的冬夜里笑声不断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 jubilant</td><td>/ˈdʒuːbɪlənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>喜悦的</td><td>a jubilant crowd<br>欢欣的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nominee gave a short speech before the jubilant crowd.  被提名人在欢欣鼓舞的人群前发表了简短讲话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>metaphor</b></td><td>/ˈmetəfər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>暗喻</td><td>a mixed metaphor<br>混杂的暗喻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speech collapsed into a mixed metaphor about ships, mountains, and rising tides.  这篇演讲陷入了船、山与涨潮混杂的暗喻之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 simile</td><td>/ˈsɪməli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>明喻</td><td>an apt simile<br>贴切的明喻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet uses an apt simile to compare memory with drifting smoke.  诗人用一个贴切的明喻把记忆比作飘散的烟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>muddy</b></td><td>/ˈmʌdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>浑浊的，不清晰的；使难以理解</td><td>muddy the argument<br>把论点搅浑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vague statistics only muddy the argument instead of settling it.  含糊的统计数据只会把论点搅浑，而不能解决争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cloudy</td><td>/ˈklaʊdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>混浊的；多云的</td><td>cloudy liquid<br>混浊的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cloudy broth needed another straining.  这混浊的高汤还需再过滤一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foul</td><td>/ˈfaʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>玷污（名誉）；弄脏</td><td>foul one's good name<br>玷污自己的好名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal fouled the good name he had spent decades building.  这桩丑闻玷污了他数十年积累的好名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 murky</td><td>/ˈmɜːrki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>昏暗的，阴暗的；晦涩不清的</td><td>a murky, windowless basement<br>昏暗无窗的地下室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The murky basement was lit by one failing bulb.  昏暗的地下室里只有一只快坏的灯泡照明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 turbid</td><td>/ˈtɜːrbɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混浊的</td><td>turbid river water<br>混浊的河水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the storm the once clear stream turned brown and turbid.  暴风雨过后，原本清澈的溪水变得棕黄混浊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 becloud</del></td><td>/bɪˈklaʊd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使模糊，使混乱</td><td>becloud the issue<br>混淆问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Emotional appeals often becloud the real issue under serious discussion.  情感诉求常常把正在认真讨论的真问题弄模糊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 befog</del></td><td>/bɪˈfɔːɡ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使模糊，使困惑</td><td>befog the argument<br>使论证变模糊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Technical jargon befogs the argument instead of making it clearer.  技术术语让论证更模糊，而非更清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 blur</td><td>/blɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>（使）变得朦胧，（使）变得不清楚；使不易理解</td><td>blur the distinction<br>模糊了界限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New technology tends to blur the distinction between work and home.  新技术往往模糊了工作与家庭的界限。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cloud</td><td>/ˈklaʊd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使模糊，使蒙上阴影</td><td>cloud the issue<br>把问题搅浑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Emotional appeals only clouded the real issue.  情绪化的诉求只把真正的问题搅浑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fog</td><td>/ˈfɑːɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使模糊，使困惑</td><td>fog the mirror<br>使镜子起雾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too much detail fogged the memory of what mattered.  过多细节模糊了对要点的记忆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obfuscate</td><td>/ˈɑːbfʌskeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使困惑，使模糊</td><td>obfuscate the facts<br>把事实弄模糊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness seemed determined to obfuscate the facts rather than explain them.  证人似乎一心要把事实弄模糊，而不是解释清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clarify</td><td>/ˈklerɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明，澄清</td><td>clarify the rules<br>澄清规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dean clarified the rules after weeks of confused complaints.  在数周混乱的投诉后，院长澄清了规则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 illuminate</td><td>/ɪˈluːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阐明</td><td>illuminate the problem<br>阐明问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her lecture illuminated a problem that had puzzled economists for years.  她的讲座阐明了一个困扰经济学家多年的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>muffle</b></td><td>/ˈmʌfl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>压低声音，包裹使消声</td><td>muffle the sound<br>把声音压低</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick curtains muffle the sound of traffic outside the window.  厚窗帘减弱了窗外的车流声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attenuate</td><td>/əˈtenjueɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>减弱，衰减；变细</td><td>attenuate the signal strength<br>减弱信号强度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick concrete walls attenuate the radio signal inside the building.  厚厚的混凝土墙削弱了建筑内部的无线电信号。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dampen</td><td>/ˈdæmpən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制，压抑（感情、精力等），泼冷水</td><td>dampen his enthusiasm<br>打击他的热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing the first game did nothing to dampen the team's spirits.  首场失利丝毫没有打击球队的士气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deaden</td><td>/ˈdedən/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使减弱，使麻木</td><td>deaden the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick carpets deaden the sound of footsteps upstairs.  厚地毯减弱了楼上的脚步声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mute</td><td>/mjuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不说话的，缄默的</td><td>remain mute on the issue<br>对此事保持缄默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained mute on the issue despite repeated questions from reporters.  尽管记者反复提问，他对此事仍保持沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soften</td><td>/ˈsɑːfən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减弱，使柔和</td><td>soften the blow<br>减轻打击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick curtains softened the noise from the busy street.  厚窗帘减弱了闹街的噪音。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stifle</td><td>/ˈstaɪfl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制（声音、呼吸等）；阻止，扼杀</td><td>stifle a yawn<br>忍住哈欠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tried to stifle a yawn while the speaker droned on.  演讲者絮絮不休，她努力忍住哈欠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suppress</td><td>/səˈpres/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>用暴力终止，镇压；封锁，删除</td><td>suppress a rebellion<br>镇压叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime used the army to suppress a rebellion in the capital.  该政权动用军队镇压首都的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amplify</td><td>/ˈæmpləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大，增强；详述</td><td>amplify a signal<br>放大信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you amplify your last remark a little?  你能把最后那句话再详述一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>mulish</b></del></td><td>/ˈmjuːlɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>mulish refusal to listen<br>固执地不肯听</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His mulish refusal to listen frustrated everyone on the committee.  他固执地拒绝倾听，让委员会所有人都很沮丧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adamant</td><td>/ˈædəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，不可动摇的</td><td>adamant about the rules<br>对规则毫不通融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was adamant that no player would skip practice.  教练坚决要求没有球员可以缺席训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 immovable</td><td>/ɪˈmuːvəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的；不可动摇的</td><td>immovable on the point<br>在此点上不可动摇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was immovable on the question of the deadline.  在截止日期问题上他毫不动摇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intractable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈtræktəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难对付的，棘手的</td><td>an intractable problem<br>棘手的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poverty remains an intractable problem in many rural regions.  贫困在许多农村地区仍是棘手的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obstinate</td><td>/ˈɑːbstɪnət/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的</td><td>an obstinate refusal<br>固执的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her obstinate refusal to compromise delayed the agreement by several months.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使协议推迟了几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pertinacious</del></td><td>/ˌpɜːrtnˈeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽固的，固执地坚持的</td><td>pertinacious in her demands<br>固执地坚持要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pertinacious reporter kept calling until she got an answer.  这位执着的记者不停打电话，直到得到答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perverse</td><td>/pərˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>反常固执的，任性的</td><td>take perverse pleasure<br>反常地感到快意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He takes perverse pleasure in arguing against every popular opinion.  他反常地乐于反驳每一个流行观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refractory</td><td>/rɪˈfræktəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倔强的，不顺从的</td><td>a refractory pupil<br>不顺从的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One refractory pupil disrupted the lesson for the entire class.  一个不顺从的学生扰乱了全班的课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stubborn</td><td>/ˈstʌbərn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，顽固的</td><td>as stubborn as a mule<br>像骡子一样固执</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandfather was too stubborn to see a doctor.  我祖父太固执，不肯去看医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flexible</td><td>/ˈfleksəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>灵活的，可变的；易受影响的</td><td>a flexible schedule<br>灵活的日程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offers a flexible schedule to parents of young children.  公司为有幼儿的员工提供灵活的日程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliable</td><td>/ˈplaɪəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的，柔软的；易受影响的，温顺的</td><td>pliable young minds<br>易受影响的年轻心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young minds are pliable and easily shaped by the adults around them.  年轻的心智易受影响，很容易被身边的成年人塑造。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pliant</td><td>/ˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易弯曲的；顺从的</td><td>a pliant assistant<br>顺从的助手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wanted a pliant assistant who would never question his decisions.  他想要一个顺从的助手，永远不会质疑他的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mumble</b></td><td>/ˈmʌmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>说话含糊</td><td>mumble an apology<br>含糊地道歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He mumbled an apology and hurried out of the crowded room.  他咕哝了一句道歉，匆匆走出拥挤的房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grunt</td><td>/ɡrʌnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>咕哝，发哼声</td><td>grunt a reply<br>咕哝着回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He merely grunted a reply and turned back to his paper.  他只咕哝了一声便又看起报纸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 murmur</td><td>/ˈmɜːrmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>murmur a complaint<br>低声抱怨一句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers murmured about the new rules but nobody complained openly.  工人们对新规定低声抱怨，却没人公开抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mutter</td><td>/ˈmʌtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨</td><td>mutter under one's breath<br>低声嘟囔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He muttered under his breath as he carried the heavy boxes upstairs.  他把沉重的箱子搬上楼时低声嘟囔着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 whisper</td><td>/ˈwɪspər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>低声说，耳语</td><td>whisper in her ear<br>在她耳边低语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She whispered the answer so the teacher would not hear.  她低声说出答案，以免老师听见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 articulate</td><td>/ɑːrˈtɪkjuleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>清晰地表达；表达清晰的</td><td>articulate her concerns<br>清晰表达她的顾虑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She managed to articulate her concerns clearly at the board meeting.  她在董事会上清晰地表达了自己的顾虑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enunciate</td><td>/ɪˈnʌnsieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、及物动词</td><td>清晰地说；公开宣布，宣称</td><td>enunciate each word clearly<br>清晰吐出每个词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please enunciate each word clearly so the people at the back can hear.  请把每个词清晰地吐出，好让后排的人听清。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mundane</b></td><td>/mʌnˈdeɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平凡的</td><td>mundane daily chores<br>平凡的日常杂务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent the morning on mundane daily chores like laundry and dishes.  他整个上午都在做洗衣洗碗这类平凡的日常杂务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 common</td><td>/ˈkɑːmən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，常见的；共有的</td><td>a common mistake<br>常见的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Spelling errors are a common problem in student essays.  拼写错误是学生作文中常见的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ordinary</td><td>/ˈɔːrdəneri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普通的，平常的</td><td>just an ordinary weekday<br>只是平常的工作日</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked like an ordinary commuter waiting for the morning bus.  他看上去就像个等早班公交的普通上班族。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prosaic</td><td>/prəˈzeɪɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，常见的</td><td>prosaic details<br>平淡的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Behind the legend lies a prosaic story about a lost receipt.  传说背后是一个关于遗失收据的平淡故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extraordinary</td><td>/ɪkˈstrɔːrdəneri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非凡的，特别的</td><td>extraordinary courage<br>非凡的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took extraordinary courage to report what she had seen.  举报她所见之事需要非凡的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>munificent</b></td><td>/mjuːˈnɪfɪsnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨的</td><td>a munificent donation<br>一笔慷慨的捐赠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The university received a munificent donation from a former engineering student.  这所大学收到了一位工程系校友的慷慨捐赠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bountiful</td><td>/ˈbaʊntɪfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，充裕的</td><td>a bountiful harvest<br>丰盛的收成</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The valley gave them a bountiful crop of apples.  这个山谷给了他们丰盛的苹果收成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 charitable</td><td>/ˈtʃærətəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，仁慈的</td><td>a charitable view<br>宽容的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a charitable view of his student's careless mistake.  他对学生的粗心错误持宽容态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 generous</td><td>/ˈdʒenərəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，大量的；慷慨的</td><td>a generous helping<br>满满的一份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She poured a generous glass of wine for each guest.  她给每位客人倒了满满一杯酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 openhanded</del></td><td>/ˌoʊpənˈhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>慷慨的，大方的</td><td>openhanded generosity<br>毫不吝惜的慷慨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The openhanded donor paid for the whole library wing.  这位慷慨的捐赠者出资建了整个图书馆侧楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsparing</td><td>/ʌnˈsperɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫不留情的；毫不吝惜的</td><td>unsparing criticism<br>毫不留情的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The review was unsparing about the actor's weak performance.  这篇评论对该演员糟糕的表演毫不留情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 closefisted</del></td><td>/ˌkloʊsˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，一毛不拔的</td><td>a closefisted landlord<br>吝啬的房东</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their closefisted landlord refused to fix the heater.  他们吝啬的房东拒绝修暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mean</td><td>/miːn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑贱的；吝啬的</td><td>a mean little room<br>简陋卑贱的小屋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family lived in a mean little room above a noisy printing shop.  一家人住在嘈杂印刷店楼上一间简陋的小屋里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 miserly</td><td>/ˈmaɪzərli/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a miserly tip<br>吝啬的小费</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He left a miserly tip after complaining about the service all evening.  他整晚抱怨服务，最后只留下吝啬的小费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 niggardly</td><td>/ˈnɪɡərdli/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，少得可怜的</td><td>a niggardly raise<br>少得可怜的加薪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered a niggardly raise after record profits.  在创纪录盈利后公司给了少得可怜的加薪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 parsimonious</td><td>/ˌpɑːrsəˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，过分节俭的</td><td>parsimonious with praise<br>极少给出赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parsimonious landlord refused to replace the broken heater.  吝啬的房东拒绝更换坏了的暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 penurious</del></td><td>/pəˈnʊriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；吝啬的</td><td>penurious circumstances<br>贫困的境况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent a penurious youth in a crowded tenement.  他在拥挤的廉租房里度过了贫困的青年时期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stingy</td><td>/ˈstɪndʒi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>小气的，吝啬的</td><td>stingy with money<br>在钱上很吝啬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is stingy with money but surprisingly generous with his time.  他在钱上吝啬，在时间上却出奇大方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 tightfisted</del></td><td>/ˌtaɪtˈfɪstɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的</td><td>a tightfisted employer<br>吝啬的雇主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their tightfisted employer counted every paper clip.  他们吝啬的雇主连回形针都要数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>murky</b></td><td>/ˈmɜːrki/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>昏暗的，阴暗的；晦涩不清的</td><td>a murky, windowless basement<br>昏暗无窗的地下室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The murky basement was lit by one failing bulb.  昏暗的地下室里只有一只快坏的灯泡照明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ambiguous</td><td>/æmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的，含义不明的</td><td>an ambiguous reply<br>含糊其辞的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract's wording is ambiguous and invites future disputes.  合同措辞含义不明，容易引发日后争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arcane</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深奥的，难以理解的</td><td>arcane rules<br>晦涩难懂的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax code is full of arcane rules nobody understands.  税法充满无人理解的晦涩规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cloudy</td><td>/ˈklaʊdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>混浊的；多云的</td><td>cloudy liquid<br>混浊的液体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cloudy broth needed another straining.  这混浊的高汤还需再过滤一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equivocal</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模棱两可的，含糊的</td><td>an equivocal answer<br>模棱两可的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave an equivocal account of that evening.  证人对那晚的叙述模棱两可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 muddy</td><td>/ˈmʌdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>浑浊的，不清晰的；使难以理解</td><td>muddy the argument<br>把论点搅浑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vague statistics only muddy the argument instead of settling it.  含糊的统计数据只会把论点搅浑，而不能解决争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nebulous</td><td>/ˈnebjələs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的</td><td>a nebulous plan<br>模糊不清的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager presented a nebulous plan with no dates, budgets, or clear goals.  经理提出了一个没有日期、预算或明确目标的模糊计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obscure</td><td>/əbˈskjʊr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>含义模糊的；平凡的，不知名的</td><td>an obscure poet<br>不知名的诗人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an obscure poet until a critic rediscovered his work decades later.  他一直是个不知名的诗人，直到几十年后一位批评家重新发现他的作品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 occult</td><td>/əˈkʌlt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>occult knowledge<br>玄奥的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manuscript is filled with occult knowledge that scholars still cannot decipher.  这份手稿满是学者至今无法解读的玄奥知识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vague</td><td>/veɪɡ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达不清的</td><td>a vague answer<br>含糊的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His vague answer told the reporters almost nothing about the plan.  他含糊的回答几乎没有向记者透露任何计划内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 limpid</td><td>/ˈlɪmpɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>透明清澈的</td><td>a limpid mountain stream<br>清澈的山间溪流</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The limpid water let us count stones on the bottom.  清澈的水让我们能数清水底的石头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pellucid</td><td>/pəˈluːsɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>清晰明确的，易懂的</td><td>pellucid prose<br>明白易懂的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pellucid explanation made the theory seem obvious.  他清晰的讲解让这个理论显得浅显易懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>murmur</b></td><td>/ˈmɜːrmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>murmur a complaint<br>低声抱怨一句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers murmured about the new rules but nobody complained openly.  工人们对新规定低声抱怨，却没人公开抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carp</td><td>/kɑːrp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>对小事吹毛求疵，挑剔，表现不满</td><td>carp at every detail<br>对每个细节都挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She carped at every detail of the seating arrangement.  她对座位安排的每个细节都挑剔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuss</td><td>/fʌs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小题大做，抱怨；忙乱</td><td>make a fuss about<br>为…大惊小怪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't make a fuss about a tiny stain on the tablecloth.  别为桌布上一小块污渍小题大做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gripe</td><td>/ɡraɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨</td><td>gripe about the food<br>抱怨饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passengers griped loudly about the delay for nearly a whole hour.  乘客们大声抱怨延误，抱怨了将近一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grizzle</td><td>/ˈɡrɪzəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨，啜泣着诉苦</td><td>grizzle about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tired child grizzled all the way home from the park.  疲惫的孩子从公园一路哭闹抱怨回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouch</td><td>/ˈɡraʊtʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨；牢骚满腹的人</td><td>grouch about the rules<br>抱怨规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grouched about the new rules but followed them anyway.  他抱怨新规定，但还是照做了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouse</td><td>/ˈɡraʊs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>grouse about long hours<br>抱怨工时太长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crew groused about long hours during the harvest season.  收割季里工人们抱怨工时太长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grumble</td><td>/ˈɡrʌmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（尤指低声地）抱怨，埋怨</td><td>grumble under one's breath<br>低声抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers grumbled about the new rules but followed them anyway.  工人们低声抱怨新规定，但还是照办了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moan</td><td>/ˈmoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀叹，抱怨</td><td>moan about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moaned about the heat for the entire drive.  整段车程他们都在抱怨炎热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 repine</del></td><td>/rɪˈpaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨，表达不满；渴望</td><td>repine at one's fate<br>抱怨自己的命运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rather than repine at his losses, he quietly started the business again.  他没有抱怨自己的损失，而是默默重新开始了生意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 whine</td><td>/ˈwaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀鸣，抱怨</td><td>whine about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children whined about the long walk to the station.  孩子们抱怨去车站要走那么远。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crow</td><td>/kroʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>得意地夸耀，自鸣得意</td><td>crow about the win<br>为获胜而得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crowed about his victory until even his closest friends grew tired.  他为胜利得意个不停，连最亲近的朋友都听厌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mutate</b></td><td>/ˈmjuːteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）改变，（使）变异</td><td>mutate into a new form<br>变异成新形态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus can mutate quickly, making last year's vaccine far less effective.  这种病毒会迅速变异，使去年的疫苗效果大减。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alter</td><td>/ˈɔːltər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>改变，修改</td><td>alter the design<br>修改设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The architect had to alter the design after the budget was cut.  预算削减后建筑师不得不修改设计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 change</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪndʒ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>改变，变化；零钱</td><td>change your mind<br>改变主意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could change his mind about leaving.  谁说什么都无法改变他离开的主意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluctuate</td><td>/ˈflʌktʃueɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>波动，起伏</td><td>fluctuate wildly<br>剧烈波动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Oil prices fluctuated wildly throughout the autumn months.  整个秋季油价剧烈波动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 modify</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>修改，调整</td><td>modify the design<br>修改设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The engineers modified the design after the first test flight.  工程师在首飞后修改了设计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shift</td><td>/ˈʃɪft/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>转变，改变；移动</td><td>shift one's position<br>改变立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public opinion shifted sharply after the televised debate.  电视辩论后民意急剧转变。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transfigure</td><td>/trænsˈfɪɡjər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使改变外观</td><td>transfigure her face<br>使她面容改观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rising sunlight transfigured the plain valley into a field of gold.  初升的阳光把平凡的山谷变成一片金色的原野。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 transform</td><td>/trænsˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>彻底改变，转变</td><td>transform the neighborhood<br>彻底改变街区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new subway line transformed the quiet neighborhood within five years.  新地铁线五年内彻底改变了这个安静的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vary</td><td>/ˈveri/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>变化，使不同</td><td>vary from place to place<br>因地而异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prices vary considerably from one region of the country to another.  价格在这个国家不同地区之间差别很大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 remain</td><td>/rɪˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>保持不变，留存</td><td>remain unchanged for years<br>多年保持不变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old rules remain in force despite the new law.  尽管有了新法，旧规则仍然有效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mute</b></td><td>/mjuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不说话的，缄默的</td><td>remain mute on the issue<br>对此事保持缄默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained mute on the issue despite repeated questions from reporters.  尽管记者反复提问，他对此事仍保持沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dumb</td><td>/dʌm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚蠢的；不会说话的</td><td>a dumb question<br>愚蠢的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Asking that in public was a pretty dumb idea.  在公开场合问那个是个相当愚蠢的想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inarticulate</td><td>/ˌɪnɑrˈtɪkjələt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达不清的，说不出话的</td><td>inarticulate with rage<br>气得说不出话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became inarticulate whenever the topic turned personal.  一旦话题转到私事，他就说不清话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 speechless</td><td>/ˈspiːtʃləs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>说不出话的，哑然的</td><td>speechless with surprise<br>惊讶得说不出话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unexpected gift left her completely speechless.  这份意外的礼物让她完全说不出话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncommunicative</td><td>/ˌʌnkəˈmjuːnɪkətɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，不爱说话的</td><td>an uncommunicative teenager<br>沉默寡言的少年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew uncommunicative after the move to a new school.  转到新学校后他变得沉默寡言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 voiceless</td><td>/ˈvɔɪsləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无声的；没有发言权的</td><td>the voiceless poor<br>无话语权的穷人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paper claims to speak for the voiceless poor.  这家报纸声称为无话语权的穷人发声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wordless</td><td>/ˈwɜːrdləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无言的，不用言语的</td><td>a wordless glance<br>无言的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They exchanged a wordless glance and left the room.  他们交换了无言的一瞥便离开房间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 communicative</td><td>/kəˈmjuːnɪkətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，愿意交流的</td><td>a communicative host<br>健谈的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew more communicative after the second cup of strong tea.  喝下第二杯浓茶后他变得更爱说话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 expansive</td><td>/ɪkˈspænsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>话多的，健谈的</td><td>in an expansive mood<br>谈兴正浓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After dinner he grew expansive and told stories all evening.  晚饭后他变得健谈，整晚讲故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loquacious</td><td>/ləˈkweɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>话多的</td><td>a loquacious guide<br>话多的导游</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our loquacious guide talked steadily from the museum door to the exit.  我们话多的导游从博物馆门口一直讲到出口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 talkative</td><td>/ˈtɔːkətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>健谈的，爱说话的</td><td>a talkative passenger<br>健谈的乘客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our talkative driver described every building along the winding coastal road.  健谈的司机介绍了蜿蜒海滨路上的每座建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>mutter</b></td><td>/ˈmʌtər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨</td><td>mutter under one's breath<br>低声嘟囔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He muttered under his breath as he carried the heavy boxes upstairs.  他把沉重的箱子搬上楼时低声嘟囔着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 carp</td><td>/kɑːrp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>对小事吹毛求疵，挑剔，表现不满</td><td>carp at every detail<br>对每个细节都挑剔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She carped at every detail of the seating arrangement.  她对座位安排的每个细节都挑剔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuss</td><td>/fʌs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>小题大做，抱怨；忙乱</td><td>make a fuss about<br>为…大惊小怪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't make a fuss about a tiny stain on the tablecloth.  别为桌布上一小块污渍小题大做。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gripe</td><td>/ɡraɪp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨</td><td>gripe about the food<br>抱怨饭菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The passengers griped loudly about the delay for nearly a whole hour.  乘客们大声抱怨延误，抱怨了将近一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grizzle</td><td>/ˈɡrɪzəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨，啜泣着诉苦</td><td>grizzle about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tired child grizzled all the way home from the park.  疲惫的孩子从公园一路哭闹抱怨回家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouch</td><td>/ˈɡraʊtʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨；牢骚满腹的人</td><td>grouch about the rules<br>抱怨规定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grouched about the new rules but followed them anyway.  他抱怨新规定，但还是照做了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grouse</td><td>/ˈɡraʊs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>grouse about long hours<br>抱怨工时太长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crew groused about long hours during the harvest season.  收割季里工人们抱怨工时太长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grumble</td><td>/ˈɡrʌmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（尤指低声地）抱怨，埋怨</td><td>grumble under one's breath<br>低声抱怨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers grumbled about the new rules but followed them anyway.  工人们低声抱怨新规定，但还是照办了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moan</td><td>/ˈmoʊn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀叹，抱怨</td><td>moan about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moaned about the heat for the entire drive.  整段车程他们都在抱怨炎热。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 murmur</td><td>/ˈmɜːrmər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>低声抱怨，发牢骚</td><td>murmur a complaint<br>低声抱怨一句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The workers murmured about the new rules but nobody complained openly.  工人们对新规定低声抱怨，却没人公开抗议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 repine</del></td><td>/rɪˈpaɪn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>抱怨，表达不满；渴望</td><td>repine at one's fate<br>抱怨自己的命运</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rather than repine at his losses, he quietly started the business again.  他没有抱怨自己的损失，而是默默重新开始了生意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 whine</td><td>/ˈwaɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>哀鸣，抱怨</td><td>whine about the weather<br>抱怨天气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children whined about the long walk to the station.  孩子们抱怨去车站要走那么远。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 crow</td><td>/kroʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>得意地夸耀，自鸣得意</td><td>crow about the win<br>为获胜而得意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He crowed about his victory until even his closest friends grew tired.  他为胜利得意个不停，连最亲近的朋友都听厌了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 delight</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使高兴，使愉快；愉悦</td><td>delight the audience<br>使观众高兴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young pianist delighted the audience with a lively encore.  这位年轻钢琴家用一首活泼的加演曲取悦了观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rejoice</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>欣喜，喜悦</td><td>rejoice at the news<br>为消息而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced at the news of the safe return.  听到平安归来的消息，全村都欣喜不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>myopic</b></td><td>/maɪˈɑːpɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏远见的，缺乏辨别能力的</td><td>a myopic policy<br>缺乏远见的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's myopic policy on housing ignored problems that residents saw daily.  这座城市在住房上缺乏远见的政策忽视了居民每天都看得见的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shortsighted</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrtsaɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短视的，缺乏远见的；近视的</td><td>a shortsighted decision<br>短视的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting research funding now would be extremely shortsighted.  现在削减研究经费是极其短视的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narrow-minded</td><td>/ˌnæroʊ ˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>心胸狭窄的，偏狭的</td><td>narrow-minded prejudice<br>狭隘的偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His narrow-minded remarks embarrassed everyone at the table.  他狭隘的言论让全桌人尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 far-sighted</td><td>/ˌfɑr ˈsaɪtɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；远视的</td><td>a far-sighted policy<br>有远见的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's far-sighted policy on parks now pays off.  该市有远见的公园政策如今见到成效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foreseeing</td><td>/fɔːrˈsiːɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有先见之明的，预见的</td><td>a foreseeing planner<br>有远见的规划者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A foreseeing manager had stockpiled parts before the strike.  一位有先见之明的经理在罢工前囤了零件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 provident</td><td>/ˈprɑːvɪdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的；有远见的</td><td>a provident household<br>节俭有远见的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A provident household sets money aside long before hard times arrive.  节俭有远见的家庭远在困难到来之前就存下钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>myriad</b></td><td>/ˈmɪriəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无限的，大量的</td><td>myriad possibilities<br>无数的可能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new technology opens myriad possibilities for teaching students in remote villages.  这项新技术为教偏远村庄的学生打开了无数可能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 innumerable</td><td>/ɪˈnuːmərəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无数的，数不清的</td><td>innumerable stars<br>无数的星星</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Innumerable stars appeared once the city lights went out.  城灯一灭，无数星星就出现了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 numerous</td><td>/ˈnuːmərəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>众多的，许多的</td><td>on numerous occasions<br>在许多场合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has warned us about this on numerous occasions.  她在许多场合就此警告过我们。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncountable</td><td>/ʌnˈkaʊntəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>数不清的，不可数的</td><td>uncountable grains of sand<br>数不清的沙粒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The beach holds uncountable grains of pale sand.  这片海滩有数不清的浅色沙粒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 untold</td><td>/ʌnˈtoʊld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>数不清的，无数的</td><td>untold wealth<br>数不清的财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shipwreck was said to carry untold wealth in gold.  据说那艘沉船载有数不清的黄金财富。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 few</td><td>/ˈfjuː/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>很少的，少数的</td><td>very few visitors<br>极少的来访者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few visitors reach the island in winter.  冬天很少有游客到达这座岛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scarce</td><td>/ˈskers/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀缺的，不足的</td><td>in scarce supply<br>供应短缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh water became scarce during the drought.  旱灾期间淡水变得稀缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scanty</td><td>/ˈskænti/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏的，稀少的</td><td>scanty evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Evidence for the theory remains scanty.  支持该理论的证据仍然很少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>nadir</b></td><td>/ˈneɪdɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>最低点，谷底</td><td>reach the nadir<br>到达最低点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His confidence reached its nadir after the third rejection.  第三次被拒后他的信心降到了最低点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 base</td><td>/ˈbeɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑劣的，低下的</td><td>base motives<br>卑劣的动机</td></tr>
